कौमारी शक्तिहस्ता च मयूरवरवाहना।योद्धुमभ्याययौ दैत्यानम्बिका गुहरूपिणी॥⁠१७⁠॥

kaumārī śaktihastā ca mayūravaravāhanā |yoddhumabhyāyayau daityānambikā guharūpiṇī ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

कौमारी · शक्तिहस्ता · च · मयूरवरवाहनायोद्धुम् · अभ्याययौ · दैत्यान् · अम्बिका · गुहरूपिणी

kaumārī · śaktihastā · ca · mayūravaravāhanāyoddhum · abhyāyayau · daityān · ambikā · guharūpiṇī

Word by word

  1. कौमारीkaumārīKaumārī, the Śakti of Kumāra (Skanda)f. nom. sg. of कौमारी (taddhita from कुमार); subject of अभ्याययौ
  2. शक्तिहस्ताśaktihastāspear in handf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies कौमारी
  3. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  4. मयूरवरवाहनाmayūravaravāhanāriding a splendid peacockf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies कौमारी
  5. योद्धुम्yoddhumto fightinfinitive (tumun) of √युध् (divādi 4Ā)
  6. अभ्याययौabhyāyayaucame forward, advancedअभि + आ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  7. दैत्यान्daityānthe Daityasm. acc. pl. of दैत्य; object of योद्धुम्
  8. अम्बिकाambikāthe Motherf. nom. sg. of अम्बिका; in apposition to कौमारी
  9. गुहरूपिणीguharūpiṇīin the form of Guha (Skanda)f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अम्बिका

Translation

Kaumārī, spear in hand and with a splendid peacock as her vehicle, the Mother in the form of Guha, came forward to fight the Daityas.

In readable EnglishKaumārī, spear in hand and riding a splendid peacock, came forward to fight the Daityas — the Mother in the form of Guha.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • योद्धुमभ्याययौ yoddhumabhyāyayau → योद्धुम् + अभ्याययौ

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    Inside the verb, अभि + आ- → अभ्या- (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

  • दैत्यानम्बिका daityānambikā → दैत्यान् + अम्बिका

    no change (final n before a vowel) · ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम् (8.3.32) does not apply after a long vowel

    The n is doubled only after a short vowel (as in एतस्मिन्नन्तरे, 8.12); after ā it is simply joined.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शक्तिहस्ता śaktihastā

    bahuvrīhi (vyadhikaraṇa: the members in different cases)

    Members
    शक्ति + हस्त
    Vigraha
    शक्तिः हस्ते यस्याः सा śaktiḥ haste yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she in whose hand is the spear”
    In the verse
    holding Skanda’s spear

  • मयूरवरवाहना mayūravaravāhanā

    bahuvrīhi containing a karmadhāraya (मयूरवर, with वर ‘excellent’ standing second)

    Members
    मयूरवर + वाहन
    Vigraha
    मयूरवरः वाहनं यस्याः सा (मयूरवरः = वरः मयूरः) mayūravaraḥ vāhanaṃ yasyāḥ sā (mayūravaraḥ = varaḥ mayūraḥ)
    Literally
    “she whose vehicle is an excellent peacock”
    In the verse
    riding Skanda’s peacock

  • गुहरूपिणी guharūpiṇī

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the possessive taddhita -इन् (feminine -इनी)

    Members
    गुहरूप + इन्
    Vigraha
    गुहस्य रूपं गुहरूपम्; तत् अस्याः अस्ति इति गुहरूपिणी guhasya rūpaṃ guharūpam; tat asyāḥ asti iti guharūpiṇī
    Literally
    “possessing Guha’s form”
    In the verse
    Skanda’s power in his likeness
Morphology & anvaya

  • शक्तिहस्ता śaktihastā

    शक्ति here is Skanda’s weapon, the spear; the same word names the goddess as a ‘power’ (शक्तिः, 8.13). The verse plays on both.

  • अभ्याययौ abhyāyayau

    Perfect of √या with two prefixes, अभि and आ: ‘came towards’ (the enemy).

Anvaya · prose order

शक्तिहस्ता मयूरवरवाहना च गुहरूपिणी कौमारी अम्बिका दैत्यान् योद्धुम् अभ्याययौ।

śaktihastā mayūravaravāhanā ca guharūpiṇī kaumārī ambikā daityān yoddhum abhyāyayau |

Subject (कौमारी, with अम्बिका in apposition and three qualifiers), infinitive of purpose with its object, verb.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Skanda’s Śakti has his spear and his peacock. She is called अम्बिका, ‘Mother’, the name the text otherwise gives to the Goddess herself: each Mother is the one Mother in another form.

Traditional interpretation

The gods praise her at 11.15 as attended by peacock and cock and bearing the great spear (मयूरकुक्कुटवृते महाशक्तिधरे).

Verse 8.18

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

तथैव वैष्णवी शक्तिर्गरुडोपरि संस्थिता।शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ताभ्युपाययौ॥⁠१८⁠॥

tathaiva vaiṣṇavī śaktirgaruḍopari saṃsthitā |śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastābhyupāyayau ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

तथा · एव · वैष्णवी · शक्तिः · गरुडोपरि · संस्थिताशङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ता · अभ्युपाययौ

tathā · eva · vaiṣṇavī · śaktiḥ · garuḍopari · saṃsthitāśaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastā · abhyupāyayau

Word by word

  1. तथाtathāso, likewiseindeclinable (adverb of manner)
  2. एवevajust, in the same wayindeclinable (restrictive particle)
  3. वैष्णवीvaiṣṇavīof Viṣṇu, Vaiṣṇavīf. nom. sg. of वैष्णवी (taddhita from विष्णु); qualifies शक्तिः
  4. शक्तिःśaktiḥthe Śaktif. nom. sg. of शक्ति; subject of अभ्युपाययौ
  5. गरुडोपरिgaruḍopariupon Garuḍaindeclinable compound (see Samāsa), locative sense
  6. संस्थिताsaṃsthitāseatedpast participle (kta) of सम् + √स्था (bhvādi 1P ‘to stand’), f. nom. sg.
  7. शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ताśaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastāwith conch, discus, mace, the bow Śārṅga and sword in her handsf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies शक्तिः
  8. अभ्युपाययौabhyupāyayaucame up, drew nearअभि + उप + आ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

Just so the Vaiṣṇavī Śakti, seated upon Garuḍa, with conch, discus, mace, Śārṅga and sword in her hands, came up.

In readable EnglishIn the same way Viṣṇu’s Śakti, Vaiṣṇavī, came near, seated on Garuḍa, bearing conch, discus, mace, the bow Śārṅga and the sword.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तथैव tathaiva → तथा + एव

    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    ā + e → ai.

  • शक्तिर्गरुडोपरि śaktirgaruḍopari → शक्तिः + गरुडोपरि

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    -iḥ before the voiced g → -ir. Inside the compound, गरुड + उपरि → गरुडोपरि (a + u → o, आद्गुणः, 6.1.87).

  • हस्ताभ्युपाययौ hastābhyupāyayau → हस्ता + अभ्युपाययौ

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā. Inside the verb अभि + उप- → अभ्युप- (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • गरुडोपरि garuḍopari

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the indeclinable उपरि as final member

    Members
    गरुड + उपरि
    Vigraha
    गरुडस्य उपरि garuḍasya upari
    Literally
    “on top of Garuḍa”
    In the verse
    riding Viṣṇu’s eagle
    Alternative
    Classical prose keeps the genitive apart (सिंहस्योपरि, 7.3); epic verse often compounds such phrases. The meaning is the same.

  • शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ता śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastā

    bahuvrīhi on an itaretara dvandva

    Members
    शङ्ख + चक्र + गदा + शार्ङ्ग + खड्ग + हस्त
    Vigraha
    शङ्खः च चक्रं च गदा च शार्ङ्गं च खड्गः च शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गाः; ते हस्तेषु यस्याः सा śaṅkhaḥ ca cakraṃ ca gadā ca śārṅgaṃ ca khaḍgaḥ ca śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgāḥ; te hasteṣu yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she in whose hands are conch, discus, mace, Śārṅga and sword”
    In the verse
    bearing all Viṣṇu’s weapons
Morphology & anvaya

  • अभ्युपाययौ abhyupāyayau

    Three prefixes on √या (अभि, उप, आ): ‘came up close’. The perfect stem is ययौ, as in आययौ (8.14).

  • वैष्णवी vaiṣṇavī

    Vṛddhi derivative of विष्णु (वि → वै, u → av): ‘belonging to Viṣṇu’, with the feminine ई.

Anvaya · prose order

तथा एव गरुडोपरि संस्थिता शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गखड्गहस्ता वैष्णवी शक्तिः अभ्युपाययौ।

tathā eva garuḍopari saṃsthitā śaṅkhacakragadāśārṅgakhaḍgahastā vaiṣṇavī śaktiḥ abhyupāyayau |

Subject (वैष्णवी शक्तिः) with two qualifiers; one verb.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Viṣṇu’s Śakti rides his eagle Garuḍa and bears his five weapons, Śārṅga being the name of his bow.

Traditional interpretation

The gods praise her at 11.16 with the same weapons (शङ्खचक्रगदाशार्ङ्गगृहीतपरमायुधे). Viṣṇu sends two more Śaktis, Vārāhī and Nārasiṃhī, from his avatāra forms (8.19–20).

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

The Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has the name वैष्णवी (name 892), there of the supreme Goddess. The two works are distinct.

Verse 8.19

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

यज्ञवाराहमतुलं रूपं या बिभ्रतो हरेः।शक्तिः साप्याययौ तत्र वाराहीं बिभ्रती तनुम्॥⁠१९⁠॥

yajñavārāhamatulaṃ rūpaṃ yā bibhrato hareḥ |śaktiḥ sāpyāyayau tatra vārāhīṃ bibhratī tanum ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यज्ञवाराहम् · अतुलम् · रूपम् · या · बिभ्रतः · हरेःशक्तिः · सा · अपि · आययौ · तत्र · वाराहीम् · बिभ्रती · तनुम्

yajñavārāham · atulam · rūpam · yā · bibhrataḥ · hareḥśaktiḥ · sā · api · āyayau · tatra · vārāhīm · bibhratī · tanum

Word by word

  1. यज्ञवाराहम्yajñavārāhamof the Sacrificial Boarn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रूपम्
  2. अतुलम्atulamincomparablen. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रूपम्
  3. रूपम्rūpamformn. acc. sg. of रूप; object of बिभ्रतः
  4. याyāwho, she whorelative pronoun यद्, f. nom. sg.; answered by सा
  5. बिभ्रतःbibhrataḥbearing, who bearspresent participle (śatṛ) of √भृ (juhotyādi 3P ‘to bear’), m. gen. sg.; qualifies हरेः
  6. हरेःhareḥof Hari (Viṣṇu)m. gen. sg. of हरि (i-stem); depends on शक्तिः
  7. शक्तिःśaktiḥthe Śaktif. nom. sg. of शक्ति; predicate of the relative clause (या … शक्तिः)
  8. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; correlative of या; subject of आययौ
  9. अपिapialso, tooindeclinable (particle)
  10. आययौāyayaucameआ + √या (adādi 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  11. तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place, तद् + tral)
  12. वाराहीम्vārāhīmboar-shaped, of a boarf. acc. sg. of the adjective वाराह (feminine वाराही); qualifies तनुम्
  13. बिभ्रतीbibhratībearing, takingpresent participle (śatṛ) of √भृ, f. nom. sg.; qualifies सा
  14. तनुम्tanumbodyf. acc. sg. of तनु (u-stem); object of बिभ्रती

Translation

She who was the Śakti of Hari, who bears the incomparable form of the Sacrificial Boar — she too came there, bearing a boar’s body.

In readable EnglishThe Śakti of Hari — of him who takes the matchless form of the Sacrificial Boar — came there too, in the body of a boar.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • यज्ञवाराहमतुलं रूपं या yajñavārāhamatulaṃ rūpaṃ yā → यज्ञवाराहम् + अतुलम् + रूपम् + या

    final m before a vowel; anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    m stays before the vowel of अतुलम्, and becomes anusvāra before r and y.

  • बिभ्रतो हरेः bibhrato hareḥ → बिभ्रतः + हरेः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before the voiced h becomes -o.

  • साप्याययौ sāpyāyayau → सा + अपि + आययौ

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha and yaṇ) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    ā + a → ā (साप-); i before ā becomes y (-प्याययौ).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • यज्ञवाराहम् yajñavārāham

    karmadhāraya (madhyamapadalopī: the middle word मय ‘made of’ understood)

    Members
    यज्ञ + वाराह
    Vigraha
    यज्ञमयं वाराहम् (रूपम्) yajñamayaṃ vārāham (rūpam)
    Literally
    “the sacrifice-boar (form)”
    In the verse
    Viṣṇu’s Boar avatāra, whose body is the sacrifice
    Alternative
    Or a taddhita on the compound यज्ञवराह ‘the Sacrifice-Boar’: यज्ञवराहस्य इदम् ‘belonging to the Sacrificial Boar’. Either way it describes the form Hari bears.

  • अतुलम् atulam

    bahuvrīhi (nañ-bahuvrīhi)

    Members
    अ + तुला
    Vigraha
    न विद्यते तुला यस्य तत् na vidyate tulā yasya tat
    Literally
    “that which has no equal”
    In the verse
    matchless
Morphology & anvaya

  • बिभ्रतः bibhrataḥ

    Present participle of √भृ (third class, reduplicated बिभर्-/बिभृ-). Reduplicating verbs have no nasal in the participle: बिभ्रत्, m. gen. sg. बिभ्रतः, f. बिभ्रती (next line).

  • वाराहीम् vārāhīm

    Feminine of the vṛddhi adjective वाराह ‘of a boar’ (from वराह); with तनुम् ‘a boar’s body’. It is also the name the Śakti bears (Vārāhī, 8.49).

  • आययौ āyayau

    Perfect of आ + √या, as in 8.14.

Anvaya · prose order

या अतुलं यज्ञवाराहं रूपं बिभ्रतः हरेः शक्तिः, सा अपि वाराहीं तनुं बिभ्रती तत्र आययौ।

yā atulaṃ yajñavārāhaṃ rūpaṃ bibhrataḥ hareḥ śaktiḥ, sā api vārāhīṃ tanuṃ bibhratī tatra āyayau |

A relative clause (या … हरेः शक्तिः) identifies the subject; the main clause (सा अपि … आययौ) has the participle phrase वाराहीं तनुं बिभ्रती.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

A second Śakti of Viṣṇu, from his Boar avatāra. The principle of 8.14 holds: as the god appears, so his Śakti.

Traditional interpretation

In the Purāṇas the Boar who lifts the earth from the waters is the sacrifice embodied (yajña-varāha). The gods praise Vārāhī at 11.17 as the one who ‘raised up the earth with her tusk’ (दंष्ट्रोद्धृतवसुंधरे).

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Vārāhī is also a warrior power in the Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa): name 76, विशुक्रप्राणहरणवाराहीवीर्यनन्दिता, ‘delighted by the valour of Vārāhī in taking Viśukra’s life’. The two battles are separate stories.

Verse 8.20

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

नारसिंही नृसिंहस्य बिभ्रती सदृशं वपुः।प्राप्ता तत्र सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः॥⁠२०⁠॥

nārasiṃhī nṛsiṃhasya bibhratī sadṛśaṃ vapuḥ |prāptā tatra saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

नारसिंही · नृसिंहस्य · बिभ्रती · सदृशम् · वपुःप्राप्ता · तत्र · सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः

nārasiṃhī · nṛsiṃhasya · bibhratī · sadṛśam · vapuḥprāptā · tatra · saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ

Word by word

  1. नारसिंहीnārasiṃhīNārasiṃhī, the Śakti of the Man-Lionf. nom. sg. of नारसिंही (taddhita from नरसिंह); subject of प्राप्ता
  2. नृसिंहस्यnṛsiṃhasyaof Nṛsiṃha, the Man-Lionm. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); construed with सदृशम्
  3. बिभ्रतीbibhratībearingpresent participle (śatṛ) of √भृ (juhotyādi 3P), f. nom. sg.; qualifies नारसिंही
  4. सदृशम्sadṛśamlike, resemblingn. acc. sg. of सदृश; qualifies वपुः; takes the genitive नृसिंहस्य
  5. वपुःvapuḥbody, formn. acc. sg. of वपुस् (s-stem); object of बिभ्रती
  6. प्राप्ताprāptāarrivedpast participle (kta) of प्र + √आप्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
  7. तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place)
  8. सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिःsaṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥshe by the toss of whose mane the hosts of stars were scatteredf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies नारसिंही

Translation

Nārasiṃhī, bearing a body like that of Nṛsiṃha, arrived there — she by the tossing of whose mane the multitude of stars was scattered.

In readable EnglishNārasiṃhī came there in a body like the Man-Lion’s, the tossing of her mane scattering the hosts of stars.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • सदृशं वपुः sadṛśaṃ vapuḥ → सदृशम् + वपुः

    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra.

  • सटाक्षेप saṭākṣepa → सटा + आक्षेप

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + ā → ā.

  • वपुः प्राप्ता vapuḥ prāptā → वपुः + प्राप्ता

    visarga sandhi (no change) · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)

    Before an unvoiced p the visarga stays.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • नृसिंहस्य nṛsiṃhasya

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    नृ + सिंह
    Vigraha
    ना चासौ सिंहः च, तस्य nā cāsau siṃhaḥ ca, tasya
    Literally
    “of the man who is a lion”
    In the verse
    of Viṣṇu’s Man-Lion avatāra
    Alternative
    Also analysed as a madhyamapadalopī karmadhāraya, नराकारः सिंहः ‘a lion in a man’s shape’. The name is the same.

  • सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (सटाक्षेप), a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (सटाक्षेपक्षिप्त) and a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (नक्षत्रसंहति)

    Members
    सटा + आक्षेप + क्षिप्त + नक्षत्र + संहति
    Vigraha
    सटानाम् आक्षेपः सटाक्षेपः; तेन क्षिप्ता नक्षत्राणां संहतिः यया सा saṭānām ākṣepaḥ saṭākṣepaḥ; tena kṣiptā nakṣatrāṇāṃ saṃhatiḥ yayā sā
    Literally
    “she by whom the multitude of stars was scattered by the tossing of the mane”
    In the verse
    her shaking mane flung the stars about
Morphology & anvaya

  • बिभ्रती bibhratī

    Feminine of the present participle बिभ्रत् (√भृ), without a nasal, as with all reduplicating verbs.

  • वपुः vapuḥ

    Neuter s-stem: nominative and accusative singular वपुः.

  • नारसिंही nārasiṃhī

    Vṛddhi derivative of नरसिंह (न → ना) with the feminine ई: ‘belonging to the Man-Lion’.

Anvaya · prose order

नृसिंहस्य सदृशं वपुः बिभ्रती सटाक्षेपक्षिप्तनक्षत्रसंहतिः नारसिंही तत्र प्राप्ता।

nṛsiṃhasya sadṛśaṃ vapuḥ bibhratī saṭākṣepakṣiptanakṣatrasaṃhatiḥ nārasiṃhī tatra prāptā |

Subject (नारसिंही) with a participle phrase and a bahuvrīhi; verb प्राप्ता.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The third Śakti from Viṣṇu, the power of the Man-Lion. The image of the mane scattering the stars gives her cosmic size.

Traditional interpretation

The gods praise her at 11.18 as the one who, in the fierce Man-Lion form, set out to slay the Daityas (नृसिंहरूपेणोग्रेण हन्तुं दैत्यान् कृतोद्यमे). In the battle she tears the asuras with her claws (8.37).

Verse 8.21

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

वज्रहस्ता तथैवैन्द्री गजराजोपरि स्थिता।प्राप्ता सहस्रनयना यथा शक्रस्तथैव सा॥⁠२१⁠॥

vajrahastā tathaivaindrī gajarājopari sthitā |prāptā sahasranayanā yathā śakrastathaiva sā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

वज्रहस्ता · तथा · एव · ऐन्द्री · गजराजोपरि · स्थिताप्राप्ता · सहस्रनयना · यथा · शक्रः · तथा · एव · सा

vajrahastā · tathā · eva · aindrī · gajarājopari · sthitāprāptā · sahasranayanā · yathā · śakraḥ · tathā · eva · sā

Word by word

  1. वज्रहस्ताvajrahastāthunderbolt in handf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies ऐन्द्री
  2. तथाtathāso, likewiseindeclinable (adverb of manner)
  3. एवevajust, in the same wayindeclinable (restrictive particle)
  4. ऐन्द्रीaindrīAindrī, the Śakti of Indraf. nom. sg. of ऐन्द्री (taddhita from इन्द्र); subject of प्राप्ता
  5. गजराजोपरिgajarājopariupon the king of elephantsindeclinable compound (see Samāsa), locative sense
  6. स्थिताsthitāstanding, mountedpast participle (kta) of √स्था (bhvādi 1P), f. nom. sg.
  7. प्राप्ताprāptāarrivedpast participle (kta) of प्र + √आप्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
  8. सहस्रनयनाsahasranayanāthousand-eyedf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies ऐन्द्री
  9. यथाyathāasindeclinable (relative adverb of manner)
  10. शक्रःśakraḥŚakra (Indra)m. nom. sg. of शक्र; subject of the comparison (supply अस्ति)
  11. तथाtathāsoindeclinable (correlative of यथा)
  12. एवevaexactlyindeclinable (restrictive particle)
  13. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of the comparison

Translation

Just so Aindrī, thunderbolt in hand, standing upon the king of elephants, arrived, thousand-eyed: as Śakra is, exactly so is she.

In readable EnglishAindrī came likewise, thunderbolt in hand, mounted on the king of elephants and thousand-eyed — in every way as Indra is.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तथैवैन्द्री tathaivaindrī → तथा + एव + ऐन्द्री

    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    ā + e → ai (तथैव); then a + ai → ai (-वैन्द्री).

  • गजराजोपरि gajarājopari → गजराज + उपरि

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + u → o.

  • शक्रस्तथैव śakrastathaiva → शक्रः + तथा + एव

    visarga sandhi and vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    Visarga before t becomes s; ā + e → ai.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • वज्रहस्ता vajrahastā

    bahuvrīhi (vyadhikaraṇa)

    Members
    वज्र + हस्त
    Vigraha
    वज्रं हस्ते यस्याः सा vajraṃ haste yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she in whose hand is the thunderbolt”
    In the verse
    bearing Indra’s weapon

  • गजराजोपरि gajarājopari

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the indeclinable उपरि as final member, containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (गजराज)

    Members
    गजराज + उपरि
    Vigraha
    गजराजस्य उपरि (गजराजः = गजानां राजा) gajarājasya upari (gajarājaḥ = gajānāṃ rājā)
    Literally
    “on top of the king of elephants”
    In the verse
    riding Indra’s elephant Airāvata
    Alternative
    As with गरुडोपरि (8.18), prose would keep the genitive separate.

  • सहस्रनयना sahasranayanā

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    सहस्र + नयन
    Vigraha
    सहस्रं नयनानि यस्याः सा sahasraṃ nayanāni yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she who has a thousand eyes”
    In the verse
    thousand-eyed like Indra
Morphology & anvaya

  • गजराजोपरि gajarājopari

    राजन् at the end of a tatpuruṣa becomes राज (a samāsānta suffix): गजानां राजा → गजराजः.

  • ऐन्द्री aindrī

    Vṛddhi derivative of इन्द्र (इ → ऐ) with the feminine ई: ‘belonging to Indra’.

Anvaya · prose order

तथा एव वज्रहस्ता गजराजोपरि स्थिता सहस्रनयना ऐन्द्री प्राप्ता; यथा शक्रः, तथा एव सा।

tathā eva vajrahastā gajarājopari sthitā sahasranayanā aindrī prāptā; yathā śakraḥ, tathā eva sā |

A main clause (ऐन्द्री प्राप्ता with three qualifiers), then a verbless comparison यथा … तथा एव.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The last of the five gods’ Śaktis: Indra’s, with his thunderbolt, his elephant and his thousand eyes. The closing words restate the rule of 8.14: as the god, so his Śakti.

Traditional interpretation

The gods praise her at 11.19 as crowned, bearing the great thunderbolt, shining with a thousand eyes, the slayer of Vṛtra (वृत्रप्राणहरे चैन्द्रि) — Indra’s own deed ascribed to his Śakti.

Verse 8.22

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

ततः परिवृतस्ताभिरीशानो देवशक्तिभिः।हन्यन्तामसुराः शीघ्रं मम प्रीत्याऽऽह चण्डिकाम्॥⁠२२⁠॥

tataḥ parivṛtastābhirīśāno devaśaktibhiḥ |hanyantāmasurāḥ śīghraṃ mama prītyā''ha caṇḍikām ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ततः · परिवृतः · ताभिः · ईशानः · देवशक्तिभिःहन्यन्ताम् · असुराः · शीघ्रम् · मम · प्रीत्या · आह · चण्डिकाम्

tataḥ · parivṛtaḥ · tābhiḥ · īśānaḥ · devaśaktibhiḥhanyantām · asurāḥ · śīghram · mama · prītyā · āha · caṇḍikām

Word by word

  1. ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
  2. परिवृतःparivṛtaḥsurroundedpast passive participle (kta) of परि + √वृ (svādi 5), m. nom. sg.; qualifies ईशानः
  3. ताभिःtābhiḥby thosepronoun तद्, f. instr. pl.; qualifies देवशक्तिभिः
  4. ईशानःīśānaḥĪśāna (Śiva), the Lordm. nom. sg. of ईशान; subject of आह
  5. देवशक्तिभिःdevaśaktibhiḥby the Śaktis of the godsf. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); agent with परिवृतः
  6. हन्यन्ताम्hanyantāmlet (them) be slain√हन् (adādi 2P ‘to strike, kill’), karmaṇi prayoga, loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana
  7. असुराःasurāḥthe asurasm. nom. pl. of असुर; subject of the passive हन्यन्ताम्
  8. शीघ्रम्śīghramquicklyindeclinable (adverbial acc. of शीघ्र)
  9. ममmamamyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; depends on प्रीत्या
  10. प्रीत्याprītyāfor (my) pleasure, out of love (for me)f. instr. sg. of प्रीति; instrumental of cause
  11. आहāhasaidthe defective verb आह ‘says, said’ (grammarians treat it as a substitute for √ब्रू in the present, with perfect endings), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; used of the past
  12. चण्डिकाम्caṇḍikāmto Caṇḍikāf. acc. sg. of चण्डिका; the person addressed

Translation

Then Īśāna, surrounded by those Śaktis of the gods, said to Caṇḍikā: “Let the asuras be slain quickly, for my pleasure.”

In readable EnglishThen Śiva, surrounded by those Śaktis of the gods, said to Caṇḍikā, “Let the asuras be slain at once, for my sake.”

Īśāna is rendered as Śiva; मम प्रीत्या, ‘for my pleasure’, is rendered ‘for my sake’.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • परिवृतस्ताभिरीशानो parivṛtastābhirīśāno → परिवृतः + ताभिः + ईशानः

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); हशि च (6.1.114)

    Visarga before t becomes s (परिवृतस्ताभिः); -iḥ before a vowel → -ir (ताभिरीशानः); -aḥ before the voiced d → -o (ईशानो देव-).

  • हन्यन्तामसुराः hanyantāmasurāḥ → हन्यन्ताम् + असुराः

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    Likewise शीघ्रं before m takes anusvāra (मोऽनुस्वारः).

  • प्रीत्याऽऽह prītyā''ha → प्रीत्या + आह

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + ā → ā. The double avagraha (ऽऽ) is an orthographic sign of the Gita Press: it marks that a long ā has been absorbed, so that the verb आह can be recognised.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • देवशक्तिभिः devaśaktibhiḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    देव + शक्ति
    Vigraha
    देवानां शक्तयः, ताभिः devānāṃ śaktayaḥ, tābhiḥ
    Literally
    “by the powers of the gods”
    In the verse
    the Mothers of 8.15–21
Morphology & anvaya

  • हन्यन्ताम् hanyantām

    Passive imperative: the passive stem हन्य- with the ātmanepada imperative ending -अन्ताम्. ‘Let the asuras be slain.’

  • आह āha

    A defective verb found only in a few forms (आह, आहुः …), perfect in its endings, ‘said’. It introduces direct speech; here the speech comes first and आह follows it.

Anvaya · prose order

ततः ताभिः देवशक्तिभिः परिवृतः ईशानः चण्डिकाम् आह — “मम प्रीत्या असुराः शीघ्रं हन्यन्ताम्” (इति)।

tataḥ tābhiḥ devaśaktibhiḥ parivṛtaḥ īśānaḥ caṇḍikām āha — “mama prītyā asurāḥ śīghraṃ hanyantām” (iti) |

A main clause (ईशानः चण्डिकाम् आह) with the participle phrase परिवृतः … देवशक्तिभिः; the quoted request is a passive imperative.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Śiva (Īśāna) is on the field, among the Śaktis, and asks Caṇḍikā to destroy the asuras quickly. He does not fight; he asks.

Traditional interpretation

Of the gods whose Śaktis came, only Śiva appears in person. His request opens the episode in which the Goddess sends him, of all the gods, on an errand (8.24–28).

Verse 8.23

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

ततो देवीशरीरात्तु विनिष्क्रान्तातिभीषणा।चण्डिकाशक्तिरत्युग्रा शिवाशतनिनादिनी॥⁠२३⁠॥

tato devīśarīrāttu viniṣkrāntātibhīṣaṇā |caṇḍikāśaktiratyugrā śivāśataninādinī ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ततः · देवीशरीरात् · तु · विनिष्क्रान्ता · अतिभीषणाचण्डिकाशक्तिः · अत्युग्रा · शिवाशतनिनादिनी

tataḥ · devīśarīrāt · tu · viniṣkrāntā · atibhīṣaṇācaṇḍikāśaktiḥ · atyugrā · śivāśataninādinī

Word by word

  1. ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
  2. देवीशरीरात्devīśarīrātfrom the Goddess’s bodyn. abl. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); ablative of separation
  3. तुtuand, then (marking the new event)indeclinable (particle)
  4. विनिष्क्रान्ताviniṣkrāntācame forthpast participle (kta) of वि + निस् + √क्रम्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb
  5. अतिभीषणाatibhīṣaṇāmost terriblef. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies चण्डिकाशक्तिः
  6. चण्डिकाशक्तिःcaṇḍikāśaktiḥthe Śakti of Caṇḍikāf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject
  7. अत्युग्राatyugrāexceedingly fiercef. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies चण्डिकाशक्तिः
  8. शिवाशतनिनादिनीśivāśataninādinīhowling like a hundred jackalsf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies चण्डिकाशक्तिः

Translation

Then from the Goddess’s body came forth the Śakti of Caṇḍikā, most terrible, exceedingly fierce, howling like a hundred jackals.

In readable EnglishThen from the body of the Goddess there came forth Caṇḍikā’s Śakti — most terrible, exceedingly fierce, howling like a hundred jackals.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • ततो देवीशरीरात्तु tato devīśarīrāttu → ततः + देवीशरीरात् + तु

    visarga sandhi; consonant (no change) · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87); खरि च (8.4.55)

    -aḥ before the voiced d becomes -o; the final t of शरीरात् stays t before t (तु).

  • विनिष्क्रान्तातिभीषणा viniṣkrāntātibhīṣaṇā → विनिष्क्रान्ता + अतिभीषणा

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

  • चण्डिकाशक्तिरत्युग्रा caṇḍikāśaktiratyugrā → चण्डिकाशक्तिः + अत्युग्रा

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    -iḥ before a vowel → -ir. Inside अत्युग्रा, अति + उग्रा → अत्युग्रा (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • देवीशरीरात् devīśarīrāt

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    देवी + शरीर
    Vigraha
    देव्याः शरीरम्, तस्मात् devyāḥ śarīram, tasmāt
    Literally
    “from the body of the Goddess”
    In the verse
    from Caṇḍikā herself

  • अतिभीषणा atibhīṣaṇā

    prādi tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अति + भीषण
    Vigraha
    अतिशयेन भीषणा atiśayena bhīṣaṇā
    Literally
    “exceedingly terrible”
    In the verse
    dreadful

  • चण्डिकाशक्तिः caṇḍikāśaktiḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    चण्डिका + शक्ति
    Vigraha
    चण्डिकायाः शक्तिः caṇḍikāyāḥ śaktiḥ
    Literally
    “the power of Caṇḍikā”
    In the verse
    the Goddess’s own Śakti, beside the gods’ Śaktis

  • अत्युग्रा atyugrā

    prādi tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अति + उग्र
    Vigraha
    अतिशयेन उग्रा atiśayena ugrā
    Literally
    “exceedingly fierce”
    In the verse
    most fierce

  • शिवाशतनिनादिनी śivāśataninādinī

    upapada tatpuruṣa (the agent suffix -इन् with the standard of comparison as first member), containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    शिवाशत + निनादिन्
    Vigraha
    शिवाशतम् इव निनदति इति (शिवाशतम् = शिवानां शतम्) śivāśatam iva ninadati iti (śivāśatam = śivānāṃ śatam)
    Literally
    “she who howls like a hundred jackals”
    In the verse
    her cry is the howl of a pack of jackals
    Alternative
    Or a bahuvrīhi in sense, ‘she whose cry is that of a hundred jackals’; the meaning is the same.
Morphology & anvaya

  • विनिष्क्रान्ता viniṣkrāntā

    Past participle of वि + निस् + √क्रम् used as a finite verb: ‘came forth’. Kālī ‘came forth’ (विनिष्क्रान्ता) from the Goddess’s brow with the same word at 7.6.

  • शिवाशतनिनादिनी śivāśataninādinī

    शिवा (feminine) is the jackal, ‘the auspicious one’ by euphemism. The word prepares the name Śivadūtī (8.28) and the ‘jackals’ of 8.27.

Anvaya · prose order

ततः तु देवीशरीरात् अतिभीषणा अत्युग्रा शिवाशतनिनादिनी चण्डिकाशक्तिः विनिष्क्रान्ता।

tataḥ tu devīśarīrāt atibhīṣaṇā atyugrā śivāśataninādinī caṇḍikāśaktiḥ viniṣkrāntā |

Subject (चण्डिकाशक्तिः) with three qualifiers; source (देवीशरीरात्); verb (विनिष्क्रान्ता).

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The gods have put forth their Śaktis; now the Goddess puts forth her own. She will be named Śivadūtī in 8.28.

Traditional interpretation

Jackals haunt the battlefield and the cremation ground, and their howls are ill-omened. The Śakti’s jackal-cry marks her as a power of the battlefield; her terrible laughter again frightens the asuras at 9.23.

Verse 8.24

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

सा चाह धूम्रजटिलमीशानमपराजिता।दूत त्वं गच्छ भगवन् पार्श्वं शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः॥⁠२४⁠॥

sā cāha dhūmrajaṭilamīśānamaparājitā |dūta tvaṃ gaccha bhagavan pārśvaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सा · च · आह · धूम्रजटिलम् · ईशानम् · अपराजितादूत · त्वम् · गच्छ · भगवन् · पार्श्वम् · शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः

sā · ca · āha · dhūmrajaṭilam · īśānam · aparājitādūta · tvam · gaccha · bhagavan · pārśvam · śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ

Word by word

  1. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of आह
  2. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  3. आहāhasaiddefective verb आह, prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada (as in 8.22)
  4. धूम्रजटिलम्dhūmrajaṭilamsmoke-grey and with matted locksm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies ईशानम्
  5. ईशानम्īśānamĪśāna (Śiva)m. acc. sg. of ईशान; the person addressed
  6. अपराजिताaparājitāthe Unconqueredf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies सा
  7. दूतdūtaO messengerm. voc. sg. of दूत
  8. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of गच्छ
  9. गच्छgacchago√गम् (bhvādi 1P, present stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  10. भगवन्bhagavanO Lordm. voc. sg. of भगवत्
  11. पार्श्वम्pārśvamto the side (of), to the presence (of)n. acc. sg. of पार्श्व; accusative of goal
  12. शुम्भनिशुम्भयोःśumbhaniśumbhayoḥof Śumbha and Niśumbham. gen. du.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on पार्श्वम्

Translation

And she, the Unconquered, said to Īśāna, smoke-grey and matted-haired: “Messenger, go, O Lord, to the side of Śumbha and Niśumbha.

In readable EnglishAnd she, the Unconquered, said to the ash-grey, matted-haired Śiva: “Go, Lord, as my messenger to Śumbha and Niśumbha.

The vocative दूत is rendered ‘as my messenger’; ‘my’ is supplied.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • सा चाह sā cāha → सा + च + आह

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

  • धूम्रजटिलमीशानमपराजिता dhūmrajaṭilamīśānamaparājitā → धूम्रजटिलम् + ईशानम् + अपराजिता

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    Both final m’s are kept and written together with the following vowels.

  • त्वं गच्छ tvaṃ gaccha → त्वम् + गच्छ

    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; so too पार्श्वं शुम्भ-.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • धूम्रजटिलम् dhūmrajaṭilam

    karmadhāraya (both members qualifiers)

    Members
    धूम्र + जटिल
    Vigraha
    धूम्रः चासौ जटिलः च, तम् dhūmraḥ cāsau jaṭilaḥ ca, tam
    Literally
    “smoke-coloured and matted-haired”
    In the verse
    ash-grey Śiva with his matted locks
    Alternative
    Or ‘with smoke-grey matted locks’, taking धूम्र with the locks implied in जटिल; the picture is the same.

  • अपराजिता aparājitā

    nañ tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अ + पराजित
    Vigraha
    न पराजिता na parājitā
    Literally
    “not conquered”
    In the verse
    the Invincible, an epithet of the Goddess’s Śakti

  • शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ

    itaretara dvandva (dual)

    Members
    शुम्भ + निशुम्भ
    Vigraha
    शुम्भः च निशुम्भः च, तयोः śumbhaḥ ca niśumbhaḥ ca, tayoḥ
    Literally
    “of Śumbha and Niśumbha”
    In the verse
    of the two asura brothers
Morphology & anvaya

  • दूत dūta

    Vocative, not a predicate: the nominative would be दूतः (दूतस्त्वम्). She addresses Śiva by his new office: ‘Messenger, go!’

  • भगवन् bhagavan

    Vocative of भगवत्, ‘O Blessed One’, with the strong stem’s n.

  • गच्छ gaccha

    Imperative second singular of √गम् with the present stem गच्छ- and no ending.

Anvaya · prose order

अपराजिता सा च धूम्रजटिलम् ईशानम् आह — “भगवन्, दूत, त्वं शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः पार्श्वं गच्छ।”

aparājitā sā ca dhūmrajaṭilam īśānam āha — “bhagavan, dūta, tvaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ pārśvaṃ gaccha | ”

The narrative frame (सा … ईशानम् आह) and the start of her speech: two vocatives and an imperative with its goal.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Goddess’s new Śakti makes Śiva himself her envoy to the asura kings. The speech runs to 8.27.

Traditional interpretation

The scene reverses chapter 5, where Śumbha sent the asura Sugrīva as messenger to the Goddess (5.102). Now the Goddess sends the highest of the gods to Śumbha — in the Śākta reading, a sign that Śiva acts at the bidding of Śakti.

Verse 8.25

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

ब्रूहि शुम्भं निशुम्भं च दानवावतिगर्वितौ।ये चान्ये दानवास्तत्र युद्धाय समुपस्थिताः॥⁠२५⁠॥

brūhi śumbhaṃ niśumbhaṃ ca dānavāvatigarvitau |ye cānye dānavāstatra yuddhāya samupasthitāḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ब्रूहि · शुम्भम् · निशुम्भम् · च · दानवौ · अतिगर्वितौये · च · अन्ये · दानवाः · तत्र · युद्धाय · समुपस्थिताः

brūhi · śumbham · niśumbham · ca · dānavau · atigarvitauye · ca · anye · dānavāḥ · tatra · yuddhāya · samupasthitāḥ

Word by word

  1. ब्रूहिbrūhisay (to), tell√ब्रू (adādi 2P ‘to speak’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  2. शुम्भम्śumbhamŚumbham. acc. sg.; the person addressed (ब्रू takes the person in the accusative)
  3. निशुम्भम्niśumbhamNiśumbham. acc. sg.; the person addressed
  4. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  5. दानवौdānavauthe two Dānavasm. acc. du. of दानव; in apposition to शुम्भम् and निशुम्भम्
  6. अतिगर्वितौatigarvitauexceedingly proudm. acc. du.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दानवौ
  7. येyewhorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. pl.
  8. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  9. अन्येanyeotherpronominal adjective अन्य, m. nom. pl.; qualifies दानवाः
  10. दानवाःdānavāḥDānavasm. nom. pl. of दानव; subject of the relative clause
  11. तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place)
  12. युद्धायyuddhāyafor battlen. dat. sg. of युद्ध; dative of purpose
  13. समुपस्थिताःsamupasthitāḥassembled, presentpast participle (kta) of सम् + उप + √स्था, m. nom. pl.; predicate of the relative clause

Translation

Say to Śumbha and Niśumbha, the two exceedingly proud Dānavas, and (to) the other Dānavas who are assembled there for battle:

In readable EnglishSay to Śumbha and Niśumbha, those two arrogant Dānavas, and to all the other Dānavas gathered there for battle:

The correlative ‘to those’ is supplied for the relative clause.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • दानवावतिगर्वितौ dānavāvatigarvitau → दानवौ + अतिगर्वितौ

    vowel sandhi (ayādi) · एचोऽयवायावः (6.1.78)

    au before a vowel becomes āv: दानवौ + अ- → दानवाव-.

  • चान्ये cānye → च + अन्ये

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

  • दानवास्तत्र dānavāstatra → दानवाः + तत्र

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    Visarga before t becomes s.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अतिगर्वितौ atigarvitau

    prādi tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अति + गर्वित
    Vigraha
    अतिशयेन गर्वितौ atiśayena garvitau
    Literally
    “exceedingly proud”
    In the verse
    arrogant
Morphology & anvaya

  • ब्रूहि brūhi

    Imperative second singular of √ब्रू with the ending -हि; the root takes the person spoken to in the accusative (शुम्भं निशुम्भं च).

  • दानवौ dānavau

    Dual: the brothers are treated as a pair. ‘Dānava’ (son of Danu) and ‘Daitya’ (son of Diti) are used for the same asuras throughout.

Anvaya · prose order

अतिगर्वितौ दानवौ शुम्भं निशुम्भं च ब्रूहि, ये च अन्ये दानवाः तत्र युद्धाय समुपस्थिताः (तान् च ब्रूहि) —

atigarvitau dānavau śumbhaṃ niśumbhaṃ ca brūhi, ye ca anye dānavāḥ tatra yuddhāya samupasthitāḥ (tān ca brūhi) —

The imperative ब्रूहि with two named addressees; a relative clause adds the rest of the host, whose correlative (तान्) is understood.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The message is addressed to the two kings and to the whole host. Its words follow in 8.26–27.

Traditional interpretation

Like a royal embassy, the message is spoken to the rulers and their assembled vassals alike: it is an ultimatum, and all who hear it are bound by their answer.

Verse 8.26

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

त्रैलोक्यमिन्द्रो लभतां देवाः सन्तु हविर्भुजः।यूयं प्रयात पातालं यदि जीवितुमिच्छथ॥⁠२६⁠॥

trailokyamindro labhatāṃ devāḥ santu havirbhujaḥ |yūyaṃ prayāta pātālaṃ yadi jīvitumicchatha ||

त्रैलोक्यम् · इन्द्रः · लभताम् · देवाः · सन्तु · हविर्भुजःयूयम् · प्रयात · पातालम् · यदि · जीवितुम् · इच्छथ
