  6. हविर्भुजःhavirbhujaḥenjoyers of the oblationsm. nom. pl. of the root-noun compound हविर्भुज्; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of सन्तु
  7. यूयम्yūyamyou (all)second-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. pl.; subject of प्रयात
  8. प्रयातprayātago away, departप्र + √या (adādi 2P), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  9. पातालम्pātālamto Pātāla, the underworldn. acc. sg. of पाताल; accusative of goal
  10. यदिyadiifindeclinable (conditional conjunction)
  11. जीवितुम्jīvitumto liveinfinitive (tumun) of √जीव् (bhvādi 1P ‘to live’)
  12. इच्छथicchathayou wish√इष् (tudādi 6P ‘to wish’, present stem इच्छ-), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada

Translation

Let Indra obtain the three worlds; let the gods be enjoyers of the oblations. You — go to Pātāla, if you wish to live.

In readable English‘Let Indra have the three worlds, and let the gods receive the sacrificial offerings. As for you, go down to Pātāla, if you wish to live.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • त्रैलोक्यमिन्द्रो लभतां trailokyamindro labhatāṃ → त्रैलोक्यम् + इन्द्रः + लभताम्

    final m before a vowel; visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    The m stays before the vowel of इन्द्रः; -aḥ before the voiced l becomes -o.

  • देवाः सन्तु devāḥ santu → देवाः + सन्तु

    visarga sandhi (no change) · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)

    Before a sibilant the visarga may stay (the alternative is assimilation, देवास्सन्तु).

  • हविर्भुजः havirbhujaḥ → हविस् + भुजः

    visarga sandhi inside a compound · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    The s of हविस् becomes r before the voiced bh.

  • जीवितुमिच्छथ jīvitumicchatha → जीवितुम् + इच्छथ

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    Elsewhere in the verse final m becomes anusvāra before consonants (यूयं प्रयात, पातालं यदि).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • हविर्भुजः havirbhujaḥ

    upapada tatpuruṣa

    Members
    हविस् + भुज्
    Vigraha
    हवींषि भुञ्जते इति हविर्भुजः havīṃṣi bhuñjate iti havirbhujaḥ
    Literally
    “eaters of oblations”
    In the verse
    the gods restored to their share in the sacrifice
Morphology & anvaya

  • त्रैलोक्यम् trailokyam

    A taddhita in -य with vṛddhi (त्रि → त्रै) on त्रिलोक: ‘the triad of worlds’, a collective neuter singular.

  • लभताम् labhatām

    Third-person imperative, ātmanepada (√लभ् is ātmanepada): ‘let him obtain’.

  • इच्छथ icchatha

    √इष् forms its present stem इच्छ- (the root’s ṣ becomes cch): इच्छथ ‘you wish’.

  • हविर्भुजः havirbhujaḥ

    भुज् is a root used as a noun at the end of a compound; consonant stem, nominative plural -अः.

Anvaya · prose order

इन्द्रः त्रैलोक्यं लभताम्; देवाः हविर्भुजः सन्तु; यूयं यदि जीवितुम् इच्छथ, (तर्हि) पातालं प्रयात।

indraḥ trailokyaṃ labhatām; devāḥ havirbhujaḥ santu; yūyaṃ yadi jīvitum icchatha, (tarhi) pātālaṃ prayāta |

Three imperatives: two third-person (for Indra and the gods), one second-person (for the asuras) with a conditional clause.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The terms of peace: Indra’s sovereignty and the gods’ share of the sacrifice are to be restored, and the asuras are to withdraw to the underworld.

Traditional interpretation

The terms answer the wrong of 5.2, where Śumbha and Niśumbha took from Indra the three worlds and the shares of the sacrifice (त्रैलोक्यं यज्ञभागाश्च हृताः), and Śumbha’s boast at 5.108. They are fulfilled at the end of the war: the gods again enjoy their shares (12.34) and the surviving asuras go to Pātāla (12.35).

Verse 8.27

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

बलावलेपादथ चेद्भवन्तो युद्धकाङ्क्षिणः।तदागच्छत तृप्यन्तु मच्छिवाः पिशितेन वः॥⁠२७⁠॥

balāvalepādatha cedbhavanto yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ |tadāgacchata tṛpyantu macchivāḥ piśitena vaḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

बलावलेपात् · अथ · चेत् · भवन्तः · युद्धकाङ्क्षिणःतत् · आगच्छत · तृप्यन्तु · मच्छिवाः · पिशितेन · वः

balāvalepāt · atha · cet · bhavantaḥ · yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥtat · āgacchata · tṛpyantu · macchivāḥ · piśitena · vaḥ

Word by word

  1. बलावलेपात्balāvalepātout of pride in (your) strengthm. abl. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); ablative of cause
  2. अथathabut, nowindeclinable (particle introducing the alternative)
  3. चेत्cetifindeclinable (conditional particle)
  4. भवन्तःbhavantaḥyou (sirs)honorific pronoun भवत्, m. nom. pl.; subject (supply स्थ or सन्ति)
  5. युद्धकाङ्क्षिणःyuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥdesirous of battlem. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of भवन्तः
  6. तत्tatthen, in that caseindeclinable (correlative of चेत्; अव्यय use of तद्)
  7. आगच्छतāgacchatacomeआ + √गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  8. तृप्यन्तुtṛpyantulet (them) be sated√तृप् (divādi 4P ‘to be satisfied’), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  9. मच्छिवाःmacchivāḥmy jackalsf. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of तृप्यन्तु
  10. पिशितेनpiśitenawith the fleshn. instr. sg. of पिशित; instrument of तृप्यन्तु
  11. वःvaḥyoursecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, enclitic gen. pl.; depends on पिशितेन

Translation

But if, out of the haughtiness of strength, you are desirous of battle, then come; let my jackals be sated with your flesh.”

In readable EnglishBut if, proud of your strength, you long for battle, then come — and let my jackals feast on your flesh.’

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • बलावलेपादथ balāvalepādatha → बल + अवलेपात् + अथ

    vowel sandhi inside a compound; consonant sandhi (voicing) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    a + a → ā inside the compound; the final t of -लेपात् becomes d before the vowel of अथ.

  • चेद्भवन्तो cedbhavanto → चेत् + भवन्तः

    consonant sandhi (voicing); visarga sandhi · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39); हशि च (6.1.114)

    t becomes d before the voiced bh; -aḥ before the voiced y of युद्ध- becomes -o.

  • तदागच्छत tadāgacchata → तत् + आगच्छत

    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final t becomes d before a vowel. (The same letters could also be तदा + आगच्छत; see Morphology.)

  • मच्छिवाः macchivāḥ → मद् + शिवाः

    consonant sandhi inside a compound · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40), शश्छोऽटि (8.4.63)

    t becomes c next to ś, and ś after the stop becomes ch: मत् + शिवाः → मच्छिवाः.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • बलावलेपात् balāvalepāt

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    बल + अवलेप
    Vigraha
    बलस्य अवलेपः, तस्मात् balasya avalepaḥ, tasmāt
    Literally
    “from the haughtiness of strength”
    In the verse
    out of arrogance in your might

  • युद्धकाङ्क्षिणः yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ

    upapada tatpuruṣa

    Members
    युद्ध + काङ्क्षिन्
    Vigraha
    युद्धं काङ्क्षन्ति इति yuddhaṃ kāṅkṣanti iti
    Literally
    “those who long for battle”
    In the verse
    spoiling for a fight

  • मच्छिवाः macchivāḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    मद् + शिवा
    Vigraha
    मम शिवाः mama śivāḥ
    Literally
    “my jackals”
    In the verse
    the jackals that attend the Goddess’s Śakti
Morphology & anvaya

  • तत् tat

    Taken as the correlative of चेत् (‘if …, then …’). The joined text तदागच्छत can equally be read तदा आगच्छत, ‘then come’; the meaning is the same.

  • भवन्तः bhavantaḥ

    The honorific भवत् is grammatically third person, but the imperative that follows is second person (आगच्छत): the politeness of the address gives way to the challenge.

  • वः vaḥ

    Enclitic form of the genitive plural of युष्मद् (= युष्माकम्); it never begins a sentence or pāda.

Anvaya · prose order

अथ चेत् भवन्तः बलावलेपात् युद्धकाङ्क्षिणः, तत् आगच्छत; मच्छिवाः वः पिशितेन तृप्यन्तु।

atha cet bhavantaḥ balāvalepāt yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ, tat āgacchata; macchivāḥ vaḥ piśitena tṛpyantu |

A conditional (चेत् …) with its main clause (तत् आगच्छत), and a closing imperative of the third person.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The alternative to withdrawal: battle, and the asuras’ flesh for the jackals. The message closes with the jackals that marked this Śakti’s cry (8.23).

Traditional interpretation

The charge of बलावलेप, arrogance in strength, is turned back on the Goddess by Śumbha at 10.3 (बलावलेपाद् दुष्टे त्वं मा दुर्गे गर्वमावह), where he accuses her of relying on others’ strength. Her answer there, that all these powers are her own (10.5), resolves both.

Verse 8.28

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

यतो नियुक्तो दौत्येन तया देव्या शिवः स्वयम्।शिवदूतीति लोकेस्मिंस्ततः सा ख्यातिमागता॥⁠२८⁠॥

yato niyukto dautyena tayā devyā śivaḥ svayam |śivadūtīti lokesmiṃstataḥ sā khyātimāgatā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यतः · नियुक्तः · दौत्येन · तया · देव्या · शिवः · स्वयम्शिवदूती · इति · लोके · अस्मिन् · ततः · सा · ख्यातिम् · आगता

yataḥ · niyuktaḥ · dautyena · tayā · devyā · śivaḥ · svayamśivadūtī · iti · loke · asmin · tataḥ · sā · khyātim · āgatā

Word by word

  1. यतःyataḥbecause, sinceindeclinable (relative adverb, यद् + tas); answered by ततः
  2. नियुक्तःniyuktaḥwas appointedpast passive participle (kta) of नि + √युज् (rudhādi 7 ‘to join’; with नि ‘to appoint’), m. nom. sg.; predicate
  3. दौत्येनdautyenato the office of messengern. instr. sg. of दौत्य (taddhita from दूत, ‘messengership’); instrument of the office
  4. तयाtayāby thatpronoun तद्, f. instr. sg.; qualifies देव्या
  5. देव्याdevyāby the Goddessf. instr. sg. of देवी; agent of the passive नियुक्तः
  6. शिवःśivaḥŚivam. nom. sg. of शिव; subject of नियुक्तः
  7. स्वयम्svayamhimselfindeclinable (reflexive adverb)
  8. शिवदूतीśivadūtīŚivadūtī, ‘she whose messenger is Śiva’f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); the name quoted by इति
  9. इतिitias (the name)indeclinable (particle marking a quoted name)
  10. लोकेlokein the worldm. loc. sg. of लोक
  11. अस्मिन्asminthispronoun इदम्, m. loc. sg.; qualifies लोके
  12. ततःtataḥthereforeindeclinable (correlative of यतः)
  13. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of आगता
  14. ख्यातिम्khyātimrenown, famef. acc. sg. of ख्याति; object of आगता
  15. आगताāgatāattained, came topast participle (kta) of आ + √गम्, f. nom. sg.; used as the finite verb

Translation

Because Śiva himself was appointed to the office of messenger by that Goddess, therefore she came to renown in this world as ‘Śivadūtī’.

In readable EnglishBecause she made Śiva himself her messenger, the Goddess became known in this world as Śivadūtī — ‘she whose messenger is Śiva’.

The passive is turned active, and the meaning of the name is added in apposition.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • यतो नियुक्तो दौत्येन yato niyukto dautyena → यतः + नियुक्तः + दौत्येन

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before a voiced consonant becomes -o, twice.

  • शिवदूतीति śivadūtīti → शिवदूती + इति

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ī + i → ī.

  • लोकेस्मिंस्ततः lokesmiṃstataḥ → लोके + अस्मिन् + ततः

    vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa); consonant sandhi (n before t) · एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109); नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7)

    -e + a- → -e: the a of अस्मिन् is elided (the Gita Press prints no avagraha here: लोकेस्मिन् for लोकेऽस्मिन्). Final n before t becomes ṃs: अस्मिंस्ततः.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शिवदूती śivadūtī

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    शिव + दूत
    Vigraha
    शिवः दूतः यस्याः सा śivaḥ dūtaḥ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she whose messenger is Śiva”
    In the verse
    the name given to Caṇḍikā’s Śakti for this deed
    Alternative
    Formally it could be a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, शिवस्य दूती ‘Śiva’s (female) messenger’; the verse’s own explanation (Śiva was made messenger by her) requires the bahuvrīhi.
Morphology & anvaya

  • दौत्येन dautyena

    दौत्य, ‘the office of a दूत’, is a vṛddhi taddhita (दू → दौ). With नियुक्तः the office is put in the instrumental: ‘appointed with (= to) messengership’.

  • शिवदूती śivadūtī

    A bahuvrīhi in दूत takes the feminine ई when it names a woman: शिवदूती.

  • आगता āgatā

    Past participle used as the finite verb, with ख्यातिम् as object: ‘she came to fame’.

Anvaya · prose order

यतः तया देव्या शिवः स्वयं दौत्येन नियुक्तः, ततः सा अस्मिन् लोके “शिवदूती” इति ख्यातिम् आगता।

yataḥ tayā devyā śivaḥ svayaṃ dautyena niyuktaḥ, tataḥ sā asmin loke “śivadūtī” iti khyātim āgatā |

A causal correlative: यतः (passive clause: शिवः … देव्या नियुक्तः) … ततः (सा … ख्यातिम् आगता).

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The narrator explains the name: she is Śivadūtī because Śiva was her envoy. The formula follows the naming of Cāmuṇḍā (7.27: चामुण्डेति ततो लोके ख्याता).

Traditional interpretation

Śivadūtī fights again in the next chapters (9.23, 9.37) and is praised in the hymn of chapter 11 (11.20: शिवदूतीस्वरूपेण हतदैत्यमहाबले).

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

The Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) also has the name शिवदूती (name 405). The shared name is a real point of contact between the two Śākta works.

Verse 8.29

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

तेऽपि श्रुत्वा वचो देव्याः शर्वाख्यातं महासुराः।अमर्षापूरिता जग्मुर्यत्र कात्यायनी स्थिता॥⁠२९⁠॥

te'pi śrutvā vaco devyāḥ śarvākhyātaṃ mahāsurāḥ |amarṣāpūritā jagmuryatra kātyāyanī sthitā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ते · अपि · श्रुत्वा · वचः · देव्याः · शर्वाख्यातम् · महासुराःअमर्षापूरिताः · जग्मुः · यत्र · कात्यायनी · स्थिता

te · api · śrutvā · vacaḥ · devyāḥ · śarvākhyātam · mahāsurāḥamarṣāpūritāḥ · jagmuḥ · yatra · kātyāyanī · sthitā

Word by word

  1. तेtetheypronoun तद्, m. nom. pl.; qualifies महासुराः
  2. अपिapifor their part, tooindeclinable (particle)
  3. श्रुत्वाśrutvāhaving heardabsolutive (ktvā) of √श्रु (svādi 5P ‘to hear’)
  4. वचःvacaḥthe words, the messagen. acc. sg. of वचस् (s-stem); object of श्रुत्वा
  5. देव्याःdevyāḥof the Goddessf. gen. sg. of देवी; depends on वचः
  6. शर्वाख्यातम्śarvākhyātamannounced by Śarva (Śiva)n. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies वचः
  7. महासुराःmahāsurāḥthe great asurasm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of जग्मुः
  8. अमर्षापूरिताःamarṣāpūritāḥfilled with indignationm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महासुराः
  9. जग्मुःjagmuḥwent√गम् (bhvādi 1P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  10. यत्रyatrawhereindeclinable (relative adverb of place)
  11. कात्यायनीkātyāyanīKātyāyanī (the Goddess)f. nom. sg. of कात्यायनी; subject of स्थिता
  12. स्थिताsthitāstoodpast participle (kta) of √स्था, f. nom. sg.; predicate of the relative clause

Translation

They, the great asuras, for their part, having heard the Goddess’s words announced by Śarva, filled with indignation, went where Kātyāyanī stood.

In readable EnglishAnd the great asuras, hearing the Goddess’s message as Śiva delivered it, went, seething with indignation, to where Kātyāyanī stood.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तेऽपि te'pi → ते + अपि

    vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)

    -e + a- → -e’-: the a is elided and marked by the avagraha.

  • वचो देव्याः vaco devyāḥ → वचः + देव्याः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before the voiced d becomes -o.

  • अमर्षापूरिता जग्मुर्यत्र amarṣāpūritā jagmuryatra → अमर्षापूरिताः + जग्मुः + यत्र

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17)

    -āḥ before a voiced consonant loses its visarga (-ā); -uḥ before the voiced y surfaces as -ur.

  • शर्वाख्यातं śarvākhyātaṃ → शर्व + आख्यातम्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā; so too अमर्ष + आपूरिताः.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शर्वाख्यातम् śarvākhyātam

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa

    Members
    शर्व + आख्यात
    Vigraha
    शर्वेण आख्यातम् śarveṇa ākhyātam
    Literally
    “told by Śarva”
    In the verse
    the message as Śiva delivered it

  • महासुराः mahāsurāḥ

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    महत् + असुर
    Vigraha
    महान्तः च ते असुराः च mahāntaḥ ca te asurāḥ ca
    Literally
    “great asuras”
    In the verse
    the asura chiefs

  • अमर्षापूरिताः amarṣāpūritāḥ

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अमर्ष + आपूरित
    Vigraha
    अमर्षेण आपूरिताः amarṣeṇa āpūritāḥ
    Literally
    “filled with intolerance”
    In the verse
    seething with indignation
Morphology & anvaya

  • जग्मुः jagmuḥ

    Perfect third plural of √गम्: reduplication ज- and the weak root ग्म्- (the vowel drops before -उः).

  • कात्यायनी kātyāyanī

    A patronymic name of the Goddess, ‘she of the Kātyāyana line’; its sense in this text is simply a name of the Goddess, used again in 11.2 and 11.25.

Anvaya · prose order

ते महासुराः अपि शर्वाख्यातं देव्याः वचः श्रुत्वा अमर्षापूरिताः (सन्तः), यत्र कात्यायनी स्थिता, (तत्र) जग्मुः।

te mahāsurāḥ api śarvākhyātaṃ devyāḥ vacaḥ śrutvā amarṣāpūritāḥ (santaḥ), yatra kātyāyanī sthitā, (tatra) jagmuḥ |

Subject (ते महासुराः) with an absolutive clause and a qualifier; main verb जग्मुः with a relative clause of place (यत्र … स्थिता).

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The asuras reject the terms and march on the Goddess. Śiva is here called Śarva; the Goddess, Kātyāyanī.

Traditional interpretation

As with the earlier messages (5.122 onward, 6.2), the answer to the Goddess’s word is indignation (अमर्ष) and attack. The tradition reads the pattern as the ego refusing the one offer that would save it.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

The Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has the name कात्यायनी (name 556). The two works share the name; their stories are separate.

Verse 8.30

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

ततः प्रथममेवाग्रे शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिः।ववर्षुरुद्धतामर्षास्तां देवीममरारयः॥⁠३०⁠॥

tataḥ prathamamevāgre śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ |vavarṣuruddhatāmarṣāstāṃ devīmamarārayaḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ततः · प्रथमम् · एव · अग्रे · शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिःववर्षुः · उद्धतामर्षाः · ताम् · देवीम् · अमरारयः

tataḥ · prathamam · eva · agre · śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥvavarṣuḥ · uddhatāmarṣāḥ · tām · devīm · amarārayaḥ

Word by word

  1. ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
  2. प्रथमम्prathamamfirst, at the outsetindeclinable (adverbial acc. of प्रथम)
  3. एवevaat once, the very (first)indeclinable (emphatic particle)
  4. अग्रेagrein front, in the vann. loc. sg. of अग्र, used adverbially
  5. शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिःśaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥwith showers of arrows, spears and swordsf. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument of ववर्षुः
  6. ववर्षुःvavarṣuḥrained (upon)√वृष् (bhvādi 1P ‘to rain’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  7. उद्धतामर्षाःuddhatāmarṣāḥtheir fury rousedm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अमरारयः
  8. ताम्tāmthatpronoun तद्, f. acc. sg.; qualifies देवीम्
  9. देवीम्devīmthe Goddessf. acc. sg. of देवी; object of ववर्षुः
  10. अमरारयःamarārayaḥthe enemies of the immortalsm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of ववर्षुः

Translation

Then at the very first, in front, the enemies of the immortals, their indignation roused, rained upon that Goddess with showers of arrows, spears and swords.

In readable EnglishThen the enemies of the gods, their fury roused, at once rained down on the Goddess from the front showers of arrows, spears and swords.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • प्रथममेवाग्रे prathamamevāgre → प्रथमम् + एव + अग्रे

    final m before a vowel; vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    The m is kept before the vowel of एव; a + a → ā (एवाग्रे).

  • शक्त्यृष्टि śaktyṛṣṭi → शक्ति + ऋष्टि

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    i before the vowel ṛ becomes y.

  • ववर्षुरुद्धतामर्षास्तां vavarṣuruddhatāmarṣāstāṃ → ववर्षुः + उद्धतामर्षाः + ताम्

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    -uḥ before a vowel → -ur; -āḥ before t → -ās.

  • देवीममरारयः devīmamarārayaḥ → देवीम् + अमरारयः

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    Inside the compound अमर + अरयः → अमरारयः (a + a → ā, 6.1.101).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिः śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa whose first member is an itaretara dvandva

    Members
    शर + शक्ति + ऋष्टि + वृष्टि
    Vigraha
    शराः च शक्तयः च ऋष्टयः च शरशक्त्यृष्टयः; तेषां वृष्टयः, ताभिः śarāḥ ca śaktayaḥ ca ṛṣṭayaḥ ca śaraśaktyṛṣṭayaḥ; teṣāṃ vṛṣṭayaḥ, tābhiḥ
    Literally
    “with rains of arrows, spears and swords”
    In the verse
    with volleys of every missile

  • उद्धतामर्षाः uddhatāmarṣāḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    उद्धत + अमर्ष
    Vigraha
    उद्धतः अमर्षः येषां ते uddhataḥ amarṣaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those whose indignation is roused”
    In the verse
    inflamed with rage

  • अमरारयः amarārayaḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अमर + अरि
    Vigraha
    अमराणाम् अरयः amarāṇām arayaḥ
    Literally
    “enemies of the immortals”
    In the verse
    the asuras
Morphology & anvaya

  • ववर्षुः vavarṣuḥ

    Perfect third plural of √वृष् (reduplication व-वर्ष्-). ‘To rain’ takes the target in the accusative: तां देवीं ववर्षुः ‘they rained upon the Goddess’.

  • अमरारयः amarārayaḥ

    Nominative plural of the i-stem अरि with guṇa: -अयः.

Anvaya · prose order

ततः उद्धतामर्षाः अमरारयः प्रथमम् एव अग्रे तां देवीं शरशक्त्यृष्टिवृष्टिभिः ववर्षुः।

tataḥ uddhatāmarṣāḥ amarārayaḥ prathamam eva agre tāṃ devīṃ śaraśaktyṛṣṭivṛṣṭibhiḥ vavarṣuḥ |

Subject (अमरारयः) with its qualifier, adverbs of time and place, object, instrument, verb.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The asuras strike first, answering the message with a volley of missiles. ऋष्टि is a sword or two-edged blade; शक्ति here is the hurled spear.

Traditional interpretation

The image of weapons as rain recurs in the next chapter, where Śumbha and Niśumbha pour arrows on the Goddess ‘like two clouds raining’ (9.9: मेघयोरिव वर्षतोः).

Verse 8.31

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

सा च तान् प्रहितान् बाणाञ्छूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्।चिच्छेद लीलयाऽऽध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैर्महेषुभिः॥⁠३१⁠॥

sā ca tān prahitān bāṇāñchūlaśaktiparaśvadhān |ciccheda līlayā''dhmātadhanurmuktairmaheṣubhiḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सा · च · तान् · प्रहितान् · बाणान् · शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्चिच्छेद · लीलया · आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः · महेषुभिः

sā · ca · tān · prahitān · bāṇān · śūlaśaktiparaśvadhānciccheda · līlayā · ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ · maheṣubhiḥ

Word by word

  1. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of चिच्छेद
  2. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  3. तान्tānthosepronoun तद्, m. acc. pl.; qualifies बाणान् and शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्
  4. प्रहितान्prahitānhurled, launchedpast passive participle (kta) of प्र + √हि (svādi 5P ‘to impel’), m. acc. pl.
  5. बाणान्bāṇānarrowsm. acc. pl. of बाण; object of चिच्छेद
  6. शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान्śūlaśaktiparaśvadhānpikes, spears and axesm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); object of चिच्छेद
  7. चिच्छेदcicchedacut (to pieces)√छिद् (rudhādi 7P ‘to cut’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  8. लीलयाlīlayāwith ease, as if in playf. instr. sg. of लीला; instrumental of manner
  9. आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैःādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥloosed from her resounding bowm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महेषुभिः
  10. महेषुभिःmaheṣubhiḥwith great arrowsm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument of चिच्छेद

Translation

And she, with great arrows loosed from her resounding bow, cut with ease those launched arrows, pikes, spears and axes.

In readable EnglishAnd with great arrows loosed from her thrumming bow she cut down, as if in play, the arrows, pikes, spears and axes they had hurled.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • बाणाञ्छूल bāṇāñchūla → बाणान् + शूल-

    consonant sandhi (n before ś) · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40), शश्छोऽटि (8.4.63)

    n becomes the palatal ñ next to ś, and the ś may then become ch (the change of 8.4.63, which a vārttika extends to follow a nasal): -ān + śū- → -āñchū-.

  • लीलयाऽऽध्मात līlayā''dhmāta → लीलया + आध्मात-

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + ā → ā; the double avagraha is the Gita Press sign that a long ā was absorbed, as in प्रीत्याऽऽह (8.22).

  • धनुर्मुक्तैर्महेषुभिः dhanurmuktairmaheṣubhiḥ → धनुस् + मुक्तैः + महेषुभिः

    visarga sandhi (inside the compound and between words) · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    धनुस् before the voiced m becomes धनुर्; -aiḥ before m becomes -air.

  • महेषुभिः maheṣubhiḥ → महा + इषुभिः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    ā + i → e.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान् śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān

    itaretara dvandva

    Members
    शूल + शक्ति + परश्वध
    Vigraha
    शूलाः च शक्तयः च परश्वधाः च, तान् śūlāḥ ca śaktayaḥ ca paraśvadhāḥ ca, tān
    Literally
    “pikes and spears and axes”
    In the verse
    every kind of hurled weapon

  • आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ

    pañcamī tatpuruṣa containing a karmadhāraya

    Members
    आध्मात + धनुस् + मुक्त
    Vigraha
    आध्मातं धनुः आध्मातधनुः (karmadhāraya); आध्मातधनुषः मुक्ताः, तैः ādhmātaṃ dhanuḥ ādhmātadhanuḥ (karmadhāraya); ādhmātadhanuṣaḥ muktāḥ, taiḥ
    Literally
    “released from the blown (resounding) bow”
    In the verse
    shot from her thrumming bow

  • महेषुभिः maheṣubhiḥ

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    महत् + इषु
    Vigraha
    महान्तः च ते इषवः च, तैः mahāntaḥ ca te iṣavaḥ ca, taiḥ
    Literally
    “with great arrows”
    In the verse
    with mighty shafts
Morphology & anvaya

  • चिच्छेद ciccheda

    Perfect of √छिद्: the reduplicated syllable has c for ch (चि-), and ch after a short vowel is doubled to cch (चिच्छेद).

  • आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ

    आध्मात is the past participle of आ + √ध्मा ‘to blow’: the bow is ‘blown’, i.e. made to resound as it is drawn and loosed.

  • प्रहितान् prahitān

    Past participle of प्र + √हि ‘to send forth’: the weapons the asuras had hurled (8.30).

  • शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान् śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān

    An itaretara dvandva takes the gender of its last member: परश्वध is masculine, so the accusative plural is -आन्, agreeing with तान् प्रहितान्.

Anvaya · prose order

सा च आध्मातधनुर्मुक्तैः महेषुभिः तान् प्रहितान् बाणान् शूलशक्तिपरश्वधान् (च) लीलया चिच्छेद।

sā ca ādhmātadhanurmuktaiḥ maheṣubhiḥ tān prahitān bāṇān śūlaśaktiparaśvadhān (ca) līlayā ciccheda |

Subject, instrument (महेषुभिः with its qualifier), two objects qualified by तान् प्रहितान्, adverb of manner, verb.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Goddess cuts every missile in the air. The word लीलया, ‘in play’, is the narrator’s mark of her ease.

Traditional interpretation

The same word marks her battles in every carita: she cuts the weapons ‘as if in play’ at 2.50 and 3.4, and breaks Śumbha’s weapons so at 10.13 (लीलयैव). The tradition reads the divine acts of creation and destruction as her play (līlā).

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

The Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) gives the idea a cosmic scale: name 648, लीलाकॢप्तब्रह्माण्डमण्डला, ‘who fashioned the sphere of the universe in play’.

Verse 8.32

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

तस्याग्रतस्तथा काली शूलपातविदारितान्।खट्वाङ्गपोथितांश्चारीन् कुर्वती व्यचरत्तदा॥⁠३२⁠॥

tasyāgratastathā kālī śūlapātavidāritān |khaṭvāṅgapothitāṃścārīn kurvatī vyacarattadā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

तस्य · अग्रतः · तथा · काली · शूलपातविदारितान्खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् · च · अरीन् · कुर्वती · व्यचरत् · तदा

tasya · agrataḥ · tathā · kālī · śūlapātavidāritānkhaṭvāṅgapothitān · ca · arīn · kurvatī · vyacarat · tadā

Word by word

  1. तस्यtasyaof it, of himpronoun तद्, m./n. gen. sg.; depends on अग्रतः
  2. अग्रतःagrataḥin front (of)indeclinable (अग्र + tas, ‘at the front’), with the genitive
  3. तथाtathālikewise, andindeclinable (adverb of manner)
  4. कालीkālīKālīf. nom. sg. of काली; subject of व्यचरत्
  5. शूलपातविदारितान्śūlapātavidāritāntorn by the blows of her spearm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with कुर्वती
  6. खट्वाङ्गपोथितान्khaṭvāṅgapothitāncrushed by her skull-topped staffm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with कुर्वती
  7. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  8. अरीन्arīnthe enemiesm. acc. pl. of अरि (i-stem); object of कुर्वती
  9. कुर्वतीkurvatīmaking, renderingpresent participle (śatṛ) of √कृ (tanādi 8), f. nom. sg.; qualifies काली
  10. व्यचरत्vyacaratranged about, moved through (the field)वि + √चर् (bhvādi 1P ‘to move’), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  11. तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)

Translation

Likewise Kālī then ranged in front of it, making the enemies torn by the blows of her spear and crushed by her khaṭvāṅga.

In readable EnglishSo too Kālī ranged at the front of the fight, tearing the foe with blows of her spear and crushing them with her skull-staff.

तस्य अग्रतः, ‘in front of it/him’, is rendered ‘at the front of the fight’; see the flag.

Textual verification / uncertain: तस्य is masculine or neuter: most naturally the enemy host (सैन्यम्), at whose front Kālī ranges, or Śiva, last named in 8.29. Many renderings take it as ‘before her (Caṇḍikā)’, which would need तस्याः; that reading treats the masculine as a licence.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तस्याग्रतस्तथा tasyāgratastathā → तस्य + अग्रतः + तथा

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha); visarga sandhi · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    a + a → ā; visarga before t becomes s.

  • पोथितांश्चारीन् pothitāṃścārīn → पोथितान् + च + अरीन्

    consonant sandhi (n before c); vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    Final n before c becomes ṃś (पोथितांश्च); a + a → ā (चारीन्).

  • व्यचरत्तदा vyacarattadā → व्यचरत् + तदा

    consonant sandhi (no change) · खरि च (8.4.55)

    t stays t before t. Inside the verb, वि + अचरत् → व्यचरत् (इको यणचि, 6.1.77).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शूलपातविदारितान् śūlapātavidāritān

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (शूलपात)

    Members
    शूलपात + विदारित
    Vigraha
    शूलस्य पाताः शूलपाताः; तैः विदारिताः, तान् śūlasya pātāḥ śūlapātāḥ; taiḥ vidāritāḥ, tān
    Literally
    “torn apart by the falls of the spear”
    In the verse
    ripped open by her spear-strokes

  • खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् khaṭvāṅgapothitān

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa

    Members
    खट्वाङ्ग + पोथित
    Vigraha
    खट्वाङ्गेन पोथिताः, तान् khaṭvāṅgena pothitāḥ, tān
    Literally
    “crushed by the khaṭvāṅga”
    In the verse
    battered with her skull-staff
Morphology & anvaya

  • कुर्वती kurvatī

    Feminine present participle of √कृ (stem कुर्वत्), with a double accusative: अरीन् (object) and the two participles (what she made them) — ‘making the enemies torn and crushed’.

  • व्यचरत् vyacarat

    Imperfect with the augment between prefix and root: वि + अ + चरत्. Contrast the augmentless उपबृंहयत् of 8.9.

  • खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् khaṭvāṅgapothitān

    पोथित is the past participle of the causative of √पुथ् ‘to crush’ (पोथयति). The khaṭvāṅga, a staff topped with a skull, is Kālī’s weapon from her first appearance (7.7, 7.15).

Anvaya · prose order

तदा काली तथा तस्य अग्रतः अरीन् शूलपातविदारितान् खट्वाङ्गपोथितान् च कुर्वती व्यचरत्।

tadā kālī tathā tasya agrataḥ arīn śūlapātavidāritān khaṭvāṅgapothitān ca kurvatī vyacarat |

Subject (काली) with the participle phrase कुर्वती (object अरीन्, two predicate accusatives); place (तस्य अग्रतः); verb व्यचरत्.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Kālī fights in the van with spear and khaṭvāṅga. The Mothers’ deeds follow one by one (8.33–38).

Traditional interpretation

The khaṭvāṅga was Kālī’s weapon from her first appearance (7.7: विचित्रखट्वाङ्गधरा), and the asuras fell to it then as now (7.15: खट्वाङ्गताडिताः).

Verse 8.33

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् हतौजसः।ब्रह्माणी चाकरोच्छत्रून् येन येन स्म धावति॥⁠३३⁠॥

kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryān hataujasaḥ |brahmāṇī cākarocchatrūn yena yena sma dhāvati ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् · हतौजसःब्रह्माणी · च · अकरोत् · शत्रून् · येन · येन · स्म · धावति

kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryān · hataujasaḥbrahmāṇī · ca · akarot · śatrūn · yena · yena · sma · dhāvati

Word by word

  1. कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान्kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryānwhose valour was destroyed by the sprinkling of water from (her) water-potm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with अकरोत्, qualifying शत्रून्
  2. हतौजसःhataujasaḥwhose vigour was destroyedm. acc. pl. of the bahuvrīhi stem हतौजस् (see Samāsa); a second predicate accusative with शत्रून्
  3. ब्रह्माणीbrahmāṇīBrahmāṇī, the power of Brahmāf. nom. sg. of ब्रह्माणी (feminine of ब्रह्मन्, ‘belonging to Brahmā’); subject
  4. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle; links this action to Kālī’s in 8.32
  5. अकरोत्akarotmade, rendered√कृ (tanādi, 8U ‘to do, make’), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  6. शत्रून्śatrūnthe enemiesm. acc. pl. of शत्रु (u-stem); object of अकरोत्
  7. येनyenaby whichever (way)relative pronoun यद्, n. instr. sg., adverbial (‘by which path’)
  8. येनyena(by whichever) wayrelative pronoun यद्, n. instr. sg.; the repetition (vīpsā) makes it distributive: ‘wherever’
  9. स्मsma(past sense)indeclinable particle; with the present धावति it gives a past meaning
  10. धावतिdhāvatishe ran, she moved√धाव् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to run’), laṭ (present) with स्म = past, prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; subject ब्रह्माणी

Translation

And Brahmāṇī, wherever she ran, made the enemies bereft of valour by the sprinkling of water from her water-pot, bereft of vigour.

In readable EnglishWherever Brahmāṇī moved, she sprinkled the enemies with water from her water-pot and left them drained of valour and strength.

The compound’s instrument (the sprinkled water) is turned into an action of Brahmāṇī; the sense is unchanged.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • कमण्डलुजलाक्षेप kamaṇḍalujalākṣepa → कमण्डलुजल + आक्षेप

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

  • हतौजसः hataujasaḥ → हत + ओजसः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    a + o → au: हत + ओजस् → हतौजस्.

  • चाकरोच्छत्रून् cākarocchatrūn → च + अकरोत् + शत्रून्

    vowel sandhi; consonant sandhi (t + ś) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40), शश्छोऽटि (8.4.63)

    च + अकरोत् → चाकरोत्. Then the final t, meeting ś, becomes the palatal c, and the ś after it becomes ch: अकरोत् शत्रून् → अकरोच्छत्रून्.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryān

    bahuvrīhi containing ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣas (कमण्डलुजल, कमण्डलुजलाक्षेप) and a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (…आक्षेपहत)

    Members
    कमण्डलु + जल + आक्षेप + हत + वीर्य
    Vigraha
    कमण्डलोः जलं कमण्डलुजलम्; तस्य आक्षेपः कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपः; तेन हतं वीर्यं येषां ते, तान् kamaṇḍaloḥ jalaṃ kamaṇḍalujalam; tasya ākṣepaḥ kamaṇḍalujalākṣepaḥ; tena hataṃ vīryaṃ yeṣāṃ te, tān
    Literally
    “those whose valour was struck down by the throwing of water-pot water”
    In the verse
    enemies robbed of their strength when Brahmāṇī sprinkled them from her kamaṇḍalu

  • हतौजसः hataujasaḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    हत + ओजस्
    Vigraha
    हतम् ओजः येषां ते, तान् hatam ojaḥ yeṣāṃ te, tān
    Literally
    “those whose vigour is struck down”
    In the verse
    drained of energy
Morphology & anvaya

  • अकरोत् akarot

    Imperfect of √कृ (stem करो-, augment अ-). With two accusatives — the object शत्रून् and the predicate हतवीर्यान् हतौजसः — it means ‘made (them) such-and-such’.

  • धावति dhāvati

    Present with स्म: a present tense used for a past action, a regular Sanskrit idiom (the particle स्म turns the present into the past). येन येन स्म धावति: ‘wherever she ran’.

  • हतौजसः hataujasaḥ

    Accusative plural of the s-stem adjective हतौजस्; for s-stems the accusative plural has the same form as the nominative plural. It agrees with शत्रून्.

Anvaya · prose order

ब्रह्माणी च येन येन धावति स्म, (तत्र) शत्रून् कमण्डलुजलाक्षेपहतवीर्यान् हतौजसः अकरोत्।

brahmāṇī ca yena yena dhāvati sma, (tatra) śatrūn kamaṇḍalujalākṣepahatavīryān hataujasaḥ akarot |

Main clause ब्रह्माणी … शत्रून् … अकरोत् with two predicate accusatives; the relative clause येन येन धावति स्म says where.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Mothers now fight, each with the emblem of the god she came from, as 8.14 announced. Brahmāṇī arrived carrying a rosary and a water-pot (8.15 साक्षसूत्रकमण्डलुः); her weapon is that water-pot. Her water does not wound; it takes the enemy’s strength away.

Traditional interpretation

The hymn of chapter 11 remembers the same act: Brahmāṇī is praised there as the one who sprinkles water with kuśa grass (11.13 कौशाम्भःक्षरिके). The rite-implement of the creator becomes a weapon in the Goddess’s hand.

Verse 8.34

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन तथा चक्रेण वैष्णवी।दैत्याञ्जघान कौमारी तथा शक्त्यातिकोपना॥⁠३४⁠॥

māheśvarī triśūlena tathā cakreṇa vaiṣṇavī |daityāñjaghāna kaumārī tathā śaktyātikopanā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

माहेश्वरी · त्रिशूलेन · तथा · चक्रेण · वैष्णवीदैत्यान् · जघान · कौमारी · तथा · शक्त्या · अतिकोपना

māheśvarī · triśūlena · tathā · cakreṇa · vaiṣṇavīdaityān · jaghāna · kaumārī · tathā · śaktyā · atikopanā

Word by word

  1. माहेश्वरीmāheśvarīMāheśvarī, the power of Maheśvara (Śiva)f. nom. sg. of माहेश्वरी (taddhita from महेश्वर, with feminine ई); a subject of जघान
  2. त्रिशूलेनtriśūlenawith the tridentn. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument
  3. तथाtathālikewise, andindeclinable (adverb of manner, here connective)
  4. चक्रेणcakreṇawith the discusn. instr. sg. of चक्र; instrument
  5. वैष्णवीvaiṣṇavīVaiṣṇavī, the power of Viṣṇuf. nom. sg. of वैष्णवी (taddhita from विष्णु); a subject of जघान
  6. दैत्यान्daityānthe Daityasm. acc. pl. of दैत्य; object of जघान
  7. जघानjaghānastruck down, slew√हन् (adādi, 2P ‘to strike, kill’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; construed with each of the three subjects in turn
  8. कौमारीkaumārīKaumārī, the power of Kumāra (Skanda)f. nom. sg. of कौमारी (taddhita from कुमार); a subject of जघान
  9. तथाtathālikewiseindeclinable (connective)
  10. शक्त्याśaktyāwith the spear (śakti)f. instr. sg. of शक्ति (i-stem); here the weapon, not the goddess
  11. अतिकोपनाatikopanāexceedingly wrathfulf. nom. sg. of अतिकोपन; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies कौमारी

Translation

Māheśvarī with the trident, likewise Vaiṣṇavī with the discus, and likewise Kaumārī, exceedingly wrathful, with the spear, struck down the Daityas.

In readable EnglishMāheśvarī struck the Daityas down with her trident, Vaiṣṇavī with her discus, and Kaumārī, blazing with anger, with her spear.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • दैत्याञ्जघान daityāñjaghāna → दैत्यान् + जघान

    consonant sandhi (n + j) · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)

    A dental n before the palatal j becomes the palatal nasal ñ.

  • शक्त्यातिकोपना śaktyātikopanā → शक्त्या + अतिकोपना

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • त्रिशूलेन triśūlena

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    त्रि + शूल
    Vigraha
    त्रीणि शूलानि यस्मिन् तत् त्रिशूलम्, तेन trīṇi śūlāni yasmin tat triśūlam, tena
    Literally
    “that which has three points”
    In the verse
    the trident, Śiva’s weapon
    Alternative
    Also analysed as a samāhāra dvigu (त्रयाणां शूलानां समाहारः), ‘a set of three points’; the bahuvrīhi suits a single weapon better.

  • अतिकोपना atikopanā

    prādi tatpuruṣa (karmadhāraya with the prefix अति)

    Members
    अति + कोपना
    Vigraha
    अतिशयेन कोपना atiśayena kopanā
    Literally
    “excessively irascible”
    In the verse
    blazing with anger
Morphology & anvaya

  • जघान jaghāna

    Perfect of √हन्: reduplication ज-घान (h becomes gh in the root syllable and j in the reduplication). One singular verb serves three singular subjects, each with her own weapon: ‘Māheśvarī struck … Vaiṣṇavī … Kaumārī …’.

  • अतिकोपना atikopanā

    कोपन ‘irascible’ is √कुप् with the agent affix -ana; feminine कोपना. With अति it is an intensive adjective.

Anvaya · prose order

माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन, तथा वैष्णवी चक्रेण, तथा अतिकोपना कौमारी शक्त्या दैत्यान् जघान।

māheśvarī triśūlena, tathā vaiṣṇavī cakreṇa, tathā atikopanā kaumārī śaktyā daityān jaghāna |

Three subject–instrument pairs share one verb and one object.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Three more Mothers fight, each with her god’s weapon: Śiva’s trident, Viṣṇu’s discus, Skanda’s spear (the śakti Kaumārī held in 8.17, शक्तिहस्ता).

Traditional interpretation

The word शक्ति is used here in its weapon sense — the spear of Skanda — beside its other sense, ‘power’, in which the Mothers themselves are the gods’ śaktis (8.13). The text keeps both meanings in play.

Verse 8.35

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन शतशो दैत्यदानवाः।पेतुर्विदारिताः पृथ्व्यां रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः॥⁠३५⁠॥

aindrīkuliśapātena śataśo daityadānavāḥ |peturvidāritāḥ pṛthvyāṃ rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन · शतशः · दैत्यदानवाःपेतुः · विदारिताः · पृथ्व्याम् · रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः

aindrīkuliśapātena · śataśaḥ · daityadānavāḥpetuḥ · vidāritāḥ · pṛthvyām · rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ

Word by word

  1. ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेनaindrīkuliśapātenaby the fall of Aindrī’s thunderboltm. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument with विदारिताः
  2. शतशःśataśaḥby hundredsindeclinable (taddhita -शस्, distributive)
  3. दैत्यदानवाःdaityadānavāḥDaityas and Dānavasm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of पेतुः
  4. पेतुःpetuḥfell√पत् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to fall’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  5. विदारिताःvidāritāḥtorn apartवि + √दॄ (causative दारि- ‘to tear’) + क्त (past passive participle), m. nom. pl.; qualifies दैत्यदानवाः
  6. पृथ्व्याम्pṛthvyāmon the earthf. loc. sg. of पृथ्वी
  7. रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणःrudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥpouring out torrents of bloodm. nom. pl. of रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिन्; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दैत्यदानवाः

Translation

By the fall of Aindrī’s thunderbolt Daityas and Dānavas, torn apart, fell by hundreds on the earth, raining torrents of blood.

In readable EnglishTorn apart by the strokes of Aindrī’s thunderbolt, Daityas and Dānavas fell to the earth in their hundreds, pouring out streams of blood.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • शतशो दैत्य śataśo daitya → शतशः + दैत्य…

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before a voiced consonant (here d) becomes -o.

  • पेतुर्विदारिताः peturvidāritāḥ → पेतुः + विदारिताः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After a vowel other than a/ā, the visarga is sounded r before a voiced consonant.

  • रुधिरौघ rudhiraugha → रुधिर + ओघ

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    a + o → au.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन aindrīkuliśapātena

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    ऐन्द्री + कुलिश + पात
    Vigraha
    ऐन्द्र्याः कुलिशम् ऐन्द्रीकुलिशम्; तस्य पातः, तेन aindryāḥ kuliśam aindrīkuliśam; tasya pātaḥ, tena
    Literally
    “by the falling of Aindrī’s thunderbolt”
    In the verse
    under the strokes of Aindrī’s vajra

  • दैत्यदानवाः daityadānavāḥ

    itaretara dvandva

    Members
    दैत्य + दानव
    Vigraha
    दैत्याश्च दानवाश्च daityāśca dānavāśca
    Literally
    “Daityas and Dānavas”
    In the verse
    the asura hosts of both clans (sons of Diti and of Danu)

  • रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ

    upapada tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (रुधिरौघ)

    Members
    रुधिर + ओघ + प्रवर्षिन्
    Vigraha
    रुधिरस्य ओघः रुधिरौघः; रुधिरौघं प्रवर्षन्ति इति रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः rudhirasya oghaḥ rudhiraughaḥ; rudhiraughaṃ pravarṣanti iti rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ
    Literally
    “raining floods of blood”
    In the verse
    streaming with blood as they fell
Morphology & anvaya

  • पेतुः petuḥ

    Perfect of √पत् formed without a reduplicated syllable: the root vowel becomes e (पेत्-), as in पेतुः ‘they fell’, नेशुः ‘they vanished’ (8.39).

  • विदारिताः vidāritāḥ

    Past passive participle of the causative वि + √दॄ (दारय-): ‘made to burst, torn open’.

Anvaya · prose order

ऐन्द्रीकुलिशपातेन विदारिताः रुधिरौघप्रवर्षिणः दैत्यदानवाः शतशः पृथ्व्यां पेतुः।

aindrīkuliśapātena vidāritāḥ rudhiraughapravarṣiṇaḥ daityadānavāḥ śataśaḥ pṛthvyāṃ petuḥ |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Aindrī, Indra’s power, who came holding the thunderbolt and riding the king of elephants (8.21), strikes with the vajra. The streams of blood prepare the next episode: Raktabīja, whose every drop of blood will become an asura.

Traditional interpretation

The chapter 11 hymn praises Aindrī as the one with the great thunderbolt who took the life of Vṛtra (11.19 महावज्रे … वृत्रप्राणहरे). Her weapon is Indra’s, and so is her history.

Verse 8.36

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ता दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः।वाराहमूर्त्या न्यपतंश्चक्रेण च विदारिताः॥⁠३६⁠॥

tuṇḍaprahāravidhvastā daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ |vārāhamūrtyā nyapataṃścakreṇa ca vidāritāḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताः · दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसःवाराहमूर्त्या · न्यपतन् · चक्रेण · च · विदारिताः

tuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥ · daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥvārāhamūrtyā · nyapatan · cakreṇa · ca · vidāritāḥ

Word by word

  1. तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताःtuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥshattered by blows of the snoutm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies the understood subject (the asuras)
  2. दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसःdaṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥtheir chests wounded by the tips of the tusksm. nom. pl. of the bahuvrīhi stem दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षस्; compound (see Samāsa)
  3. वाराहमूर्त्याvārāhamūrtyāby the one in boar form, Vārāhīf. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); agent of the participles
  4. न्यपतन्nyapatanfell downनि + √पत् (bhvādi, 1P), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  5. चक्रेणcakreṇaby the discusn. instr. sg. of चक्र; instrument with विदारिताः
  6. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  7. विदारिताःvidāritāḥtorn apartवि + √दॄ (causative) + क्त, m. nom. pl.

Translation

Shattered by blows of the snout, their chests wounded by the tips of the tusks, and torn apart by the discus, by the one in boar form, (the asuras) fell.

In readable EnglishShattered by the blows of her snout, their chests gored by the tips of her tusks, torn apart by her discus, the asuras fell before Vārāhī.

‘Her’ is supplied: all three weapons are taken as Vārāhī’s (see Commentary).

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • विध्वस्ता दंष्ट्रा vidhvastā daṃṣṭrā → विध्वस्ताः + दंष्ट्रा…

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    -āḥ before a voiced consonant loses its visarga.

  • न्यपतंश्चक्रेण nyapataṃścakreṇa → न्यपतन् + चक्रेण

    consonant sandhi (n + c) · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)

    Final n before c becomes anusvāra + ś: न्यपतन् चक्रेण → न्यपतंश्चक्रेण.

  • न्यपतन् nyapatan → नि + अपतन्

    vowel sandhi (prefix + augmented verb) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    The i of नि becomes y before the augment अ-.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताः tuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥ

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (तुण्डप्रहार)

    Members
    तुण्ड + प्रहार + विध्वस्त
    Vigraha
    तुण्डस्य प्रहारः तुण्डप्रहारः; तेन विध्वस्ताः tuṇḍasya prahāraḥ tuṇḍaprahāraḥ; tena vidhvastāḥ
    Literally
    “destroyed by the blow of the snout”
    In the verse
    smashed by the boar’s snout

  • दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (दंष्ट्राग्र) and a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (…क्षत)

    Members
    दंष्ट्रा + अग्र + क्षत + वक्षस्
    Vigraha
    दंष्ट्रायाः अग्रं दंष्ट्राग्रम्; दंष्ट्राग्रेण क्षतं वक्षः येषां ते daṃṣṭrāyāḥ agraṃ daṃṣṭrāgram; daṃṣṭrāgreṇa kṣataṃ vakṣaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those whose chests are wounded by the tusk-tip”
    In the verse
    gored in the chest

  • वाराहमूर्त्या vārāhamūrtyā

    karmadhāraya (the feminine adjective takes its masculine form as first member)

    Members
    वाराह + मूर्ति
    Vigraha
    वाराही मूर्तिः वाराहमूर्तिः, तया vārāhī mūrtiḥ vārāhamūrtiḥ, tayā
    Literally
    “by the boar form”
    In the verse
    by Vārāhī, the power of the Boar
    Alternative
    Or bahuvrīhi: वाराही मूर्तिः यस्याः सा, तया — ‘by her whose form is the boar’s’. Either way the agent is Vārāhī.
Morphology & anvaya

  • न्यपतन् nyapatan

    Imperfect of नि + √पत्: the augment अ- stands between the prefix and the root (नि-अ-पतन्), so the prefix’s i turns to y.

  • दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ

    A bahuvrīhi ending in the s-stem वक्षस् ‘chest’: nominative plural -वक्षसः, ‘(those) whose chests …’.

Anvaya · prose order

वाराहमूर्त्या तुण्डप्रहारविध्वस्ताः दंष्ट्राग्रक्षतवक्षसः चक्रेण च विदारिताः (असुराः) न्यपतन्।

vārāhamūrtyā tuṇḍaprahāravidhvastāḥ daṃṣṭrāgrakṣatavakṣasaḥ cakreṇa ca vidāritāḥ (asurāḥ) nyapatan |

The subject (the asuras) is understood; three descriptions in the nominative plural, all with the agent वाराहमूर्त्या, lead to the verb न्यपतन्.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Vārāhī, who came in the form of the sacrificial Boar (8.19), fights as a boar: snout and tusks. The discus in the same sentence is most naturally hers too, though Vaiṣṇavī has just used a discus in 8.34.

Traditional interpretation

The chapter 11 hymn gives the boar-formed Goddess a great discus and the tusk that lifted the earth (11.17 गृहीतोग्रमहाचक्रे दंष्ट्रोद्धृतवसुंधरे), which supports reading the discus here as Vārāhī’s.

Verse 8.37

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

नखैर्विदारितांश्चान्यान् भक्षयन्ती महासुरान्।नारसिंही चचाराजौ नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा॥⁠३७⁠॥

nakhairvidāritāṃścānyān bhakṣayantī mahāsurān |nārasiṃhī cacārājau nādāpūrṇadigambarā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

नखैः · विदारितान् · च · अन्यान् · भक्षयन्ती · महासुरान्नारसिंही · चचार · आजौ · नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा

nakhaiḥ · vidāritān · ca · anyān · bhakṣayantī · mahāsurānnārasiṃhī · cacāra · ājau · nādāpūrṇadigambarā

Word by word

  1. नखैःnakhaiḥwith (her) clawsm. instr. pl. of नख; instrument with विदारितान्
  2. विदारितान्vidāritāntorn apartवि + √दॄ (causative) + क्त, m. acc. pl.; qualifies महासुरान्
  3. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle; links this to the preceding verse
  4. अन्यान्anyānotherpronominal adjective अन्य, m. acc. pl.; qualifies महासुरान्
  5. भक्षयन्तीbhakṣayantīdevouring√भक्ष् (curādi, 10 ‘to eat’) + शतृ (present participle), f. nom. sg.; qualifies नारसिंही
  6. महासुरान्mahāsurāngreat asurasm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); object of भक्षयन्ती
  7. नारसिंहीnārasiṃhīNārasiṃhī, the power of the Man-Lionf. nom. sg. of नारसिंही (taddhita from नरसिंह); subject
  8. चचारcacāraroamed, moved about√चर् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to move’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  9. आजौājauin the battlem. loc. sg. of आजि (i-stem)
  10. नादापूर्णदिगम्बराnādāpūrṇadigambarāshe who filled the quarters and the sky with her roarf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies नारसिंही

Translation

And devouring other great asuras torn apart by her claws, Nārasiṃhī, who filled the quarters and the sky with her roar, roamed in the battle.

In readable EnglishNārasiṃhī ranged over the field, filling the quarters and the sky with her roar, tearing other great asuras with her claws and devouring them.

The participle विदारितान् (‘torn’) is rendered as a second action of Nārasiṃhī; the Sanskrit makes the asuras the object of one verb, भक्षयन्ती.

Textual verification / uncertain: अन्यान् can also be read apart from विदारितान् — ‘(those) torn by her claws, and others too’, both devoured. The sense differs little; the reading of the text is not in doubt.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • नखैर्विदारितांश्चान्यान् nakhairvidāritāṃścānyān → नखैः + विदारितान् + च + अन्यान्

    visarga sandhi; consonant sandhi (n + c); vowel sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    नखैः before v is sounded नखैर्; विदारितान् before च becomes विदारितांश्; च + अन्यान् → चान्यान्.

  • चचाराजौ cacārājau → चचार + आजौ

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

  • नादापूर्ण nādāpūrṇa → नाद + आपूर्ण

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā. Further on, दिक् + अम्बर → दिगम्बर: a final stop is voiced before a vowel (झलां जशोऽन्ते, 8.2.39).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • महासुरान् mahāsurān

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    महत् + असुर
    Vigraha
    महान्तः असुराः, तान् mahāntaḥ asurāḥ, tān
    Literally
    “great asuras”
    In the verse
    the mightiest of the demons

  • नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा nādāpūrṇadigambarā

    bahuvrīhi containing a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (नादापूर्ण) and a samāhāra dvandva (दिगम्बर)

    Members
    नाद + आपूर्ण + दिश् + अम्बर
    Vigraha
    दिशश्च अम्बरं च दिगम्बरम्; नादेन आपूर्णं दिगम्बरं यया सा diśaśca ambaraṃ ca digambaram; nādena āpūrṇaṃ digambaraṃ yayā sā
    Literally
    “she by whom the quarters and sky are filled with roaring”
    In the verse
    her roars filled every direction and the heavens
Morphology & anvaya

  • भक्षयन्ती bhakṣayantī

    Present participle of √भक्ष् (stem भक्षय-), feminine in -ई with the nasal: भक्षयन्ती ‘devouring’. It governs the accusative महासुरान्.

  • चचार cacāra

    Perfect of √चर्, reduplicated च-चार: ‘she moved about’.

  • आजौ ājau

    Locative singular of the i-stem आजि ‘contest, battle’: -इ + locative → -औ.

  • नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा nādāpūrṇadigambarā

    Read as one bahuvrīhi: ‘(she) by whom quarters-and-sky were filled with roaring’. Its last members, taken alone, would spell दिगम्बरा ‘sky-clad’, an epithet used elsewhere of Kālī; that is not the sense here.

Anvaya · prose order

नादापूर्णदिगम्बरा नारसिंही च नखैः विदारितान् अन्यान् महासुरान् भक्षयन्ती आजौ चचार।

nādāpūrṇadigambarā nārasiṃhī ca nakhaiḥ vidāritān anyān mahāsurān bhakṣayantī ājau cacāra |

Subject नारसिंही with her epithet; the participle phrase भक्षयन्ती with its object; the verb चचार with its place आजौ.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Nārasiṃhī, who came in a body like the Man-Lion’s, scattering the stars with a toss of her mane (8.20), fights as a lion fights: with claws, teeth and roar. ‘Other’ (अन्यान्) sets her victims apart from those Vārāhī has just struck.

Traditional interpretation

The chapter 11 hymn remembers her as the Goddess in the fierce Man-Lion form who set herself to kill the Daityas (11.18 नृसिंहरूपेणोग्रेण हन्तुं दैत्यान् कृतोद्यमे). Here, as in the hymn, she is named only by her form.

Verse 8.38

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

चण्डाट्टहासैरसुराः शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः।पेतुः पृथिव्यां पतितांस्तांश्चखादाथ सा तदा॥⁠३८⁠॥

caṇḍāṭṭahāsairasurāḥ śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ |petuḥ pṛthivyāṃ patitāṃstāṃścakhādātha sā tadā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

चण्डाट्टहासैः · असुराः · शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताःपेतुः · पृथिव्याम् · पतितान् · तान् · चखाद · अथ · सा · तदा

caṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥ · asurāḥ · śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥpetuḥ · pṛthivyām · patitān · tān · cakhāda · atha · sā · tadā

Word by word

  1. चण्डाट्टहासैःcaṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥby (her) fierce loud laughsm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument with अभिदूषिताः
  2. असुराःasurāḥthe asurasm. nom. pl. of असुर; subject of पेतुः
  3. शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताःśivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥstricken by Śivadūtīm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies असुराः
  4. पेतुःpetuḥfell√पत् (bhvādi, 1P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  5. पृथिव्याम्pṛthivyāmon the earthf. loc. sg. of पृथिवी
  6. पतितान्patitānfallen√पत् + क्त (past participle), m. acc. pl.; qualifies तान्
  7. तान्tānthempronoun तद्, m. acc. pl.; object of चखाद
  8. चखादcakhādadevoured√खाद् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to eat’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  9. अथathathenindeclinable (particle of sequence)
  10. साsāshe (Śivadūtī)pronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of चखाद
  11. तदाtadāat that timeindeclinable (adverb of time)

Translation

The asuras, assailed by Śivadūtī with fierce loud laughs, fell on the earth; and then she at that time devoured them as they lay fallen.

In readable EnglishStruck down by Śivadūtī’s fierce peals of laughter, the asuras fell to the ground, and there she devoured them.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • चण्डाट्टहासैरसुराः caṇḍāṭṭahāsairasurāḥ → चण्डाट्टहासैः + असुराः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After ai the visarga is sounded r before a vowel.

  • शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ → शिवदूती + अभिदूषिताः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    ī becomes y before a.

  • पतितांस्तांश्चखादाथ patitāṃstāṃścakhādātha → पतितान् + तान् + चखाद + अथ

    consonant sandhi (n + t, n + c); vowel sandhi · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7); स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    Final n becomes anusvāra + s before t (पतितांस्तान्) and anusvāra + ś before c (तांश्चखाद); चखाद + अथ → चखादाथ.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • चण्डाट्टहासैः caṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥ

    karmadhāraya (अट्टहास ‘loud laughter’ is itself a karmadhāraya: अट्टः हासः)

    Members
    चण्ड + अट्टहास
    Vigraha
    चण्डाः अट्टहासाः, तैः caṇḍāḥ aṭṭahāsāḥ, taiḥ
    Literally
    “by fierce loud laughs”
    In the verse
    by her terrifying peals of laughter

  • शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa

    Members
    शिवदूती + अभिदूषित
    Vigraha
    शिवदूत्या अभिदूषिताः śivadūtyā abhidūṣitāḥ
    Literally
    “assailed by Śivadūtī”
    In the verse
    struck down by Śivadūtī’s laughter
    Alternative
    शिवदूती could be a separate nominative (‘Śivadūtī …’), but then अभिदूषिताः has no agent and सा in the second line repeats her; the compound fits better.
Morphology & anvaya

  • चखाद cakhāda

    Perfect of √खाद्, reduplicated च-खाद (kh → c in the reduplication).

  • शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ

    अभिदूषित: अभि + causative of √दुष् (दूषय-) + क्त, ‘spoiled, harmed, assailed’. Here: overwhelmed by the laughter.

Anvaya · prose order

शिवदूत्यभिदूषिताः असुराः चण्डाट्टहासैः पृथिव्यां पेतुः; अथ तदा सा पतितान् तान् चखाद।

śivadūtyabhidūṣitāḥ asurāḥ caṇḍāṭṭahāsaiḥ pṛthivyāṃ petuḥ; atha tadā sā patitān tān cakhāda |

Two clauses: the asuras fell; then she devoured them.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Śivadūtī is the power that came out of Caṇḍikā’s own body with the cry of a hundred jackals (8.23) and sent Śiva as her messenger (8.28). Her weapon is her laughter: it fells the asuras, and she eats the fallen.

Traditional interpretation

The same terrible laughter returns in the next battle (9.23 अट्टाट्टहासमशिवं शिवदूती चकार ह), and the hymn of chapter 11 salutes her as Śivadūtī who slew the mighty Daityas (11.20).

Verse 8.39

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

इति मातृगणं क्रुद्धं मर्दयन्तं महासुरान्।दृष्ट्वाभ्युपायैर्विविधैर्नेशुर्देवारिसैनिकाः॥⁠३९⁠॥

iti mātṛgaṇaṃ kruddhaṃ mardayantaṃ mahāsurān |dṛṣṭvābhyupāyairvividhairneśurdevārisainikāḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

इति · मातृगणम् · क्रुद्धम् · मर्दयन्तम् · महासुरान्दृष्ट्वा · अभ्युपायैः · विविधैः · नेशुः · देवारिसैनिकाः

iti · mātṛgaṇam · kruddham · mardayantam · mahāsurāndṛṣṭvā · abhyupāyaiḥ · vividhaiḥ · neśuḥ · devārisainikāḥ

Word by word

  1. इतिitithusindeclinable; sums up the scenes just described
  2. मातृगणम्mātṛgaṇamthe band of Mothersm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of दृष्ट्वा
  3. क्रुद्धम्kruddhamenraged√क्रुध् (divādi, 4P ‘to be angry’) + क्त, m. acc. sg.; qualifies मातृगणम्
  4. मर्दयन्तम्mardayantamcrushing√मृद् (causative stem मर्दय- ‘to crush’) + शतृ (present participle), m. acc. sg.; qualifies मातृगणम्
  5. महासुरान्mahāsurānthe great asurasm. acc. pl.; compound (karmadhāraya, as in 8.37); object of मर्दयन्तम्
  6. दृष्ट्वाdṛṣṭvāhaving seen√दृश् (bhvādi, 1P) + क्त्वा (absolutive)
  7. अभ्युपायैःabhyupāyaiḥby (various) means, in (various) waysm. instr. pl. of अभ्युपाय (अभि + उप + √इ ‘approach, expedient’)
  8. विविधैःvividhaiḥvariousm. instr. pl. of विविध; qualifies अभ्युपायैः
  9. नेशुःneśuḥvanished, fled√नश् (divādi, 4P ‘to be lost, disappear, perish’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  10. देवारिसैनिकाःdevārisainikāḥthe soldiers of the gods’ enemiesm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of नेशुः

Translation

Seeing thus the band of Mothers, enraged, crushing the great asuras, the soldiers of the gods’ enemies vanished by various means.

In readable EnglishWhen they saw the furious band of Mothers crushing the great asuras, the soldiers of the gods’ enemies slipped away, each however he could.

नेशुः is taken as ‘vanished, fled’ (see morph); ‘each however he could’ renders अभ्युपायैर्विविधैः.

Textual verification / uncertain: नेशुः may also be rendered ‘perished (in various ways)’. The next verse speaks of the Daityas as ‘intent on flight’, so ‘vanished, fled’ is preferred.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • दृष्ट्वाभ्युपायैर्विविधैर्नेशुर्देवारि dṛṣṭvābhyupāyairvividhairneśurdevāri → दृष्ट्वा + अभ्युपायैः + विविधैः + नेशुः + देवारि…

    vowel sandhi; visarga sandhi · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    ā + a → ā; then three visargas after ai, ai and u are each sounded r before a voiced consonant (v, n, d).

  • देवारि devāri → देव + अरि

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • मातृगणम् mātṛgaṇam

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    मातृ + गण
    Vigraha
    मातॄणां गणः, तम् mātṝṇāṃ gaṇaḥ, tam
    Literally
    “the host of the Mothers”
    In the verse
    the Mātṛkās fighting together

  • देवारिसैनिकाः devārisainikāḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (देवारि)

    Members
    देव + अरि + सैनिक
    Vigraha
    देवानाम् अरयः देवारयः; देवारीणां सैनिकाः devānām arayaḥ devārayaḥ; devārīṇāṃ sainikāḥ
    Literally
    “the soldiers of the enemies of the gods”
    In the verse
    the rank and file of the asura army
Morphology & anvaya

  • नेशुः neśuḥ

    Perfect of √नश्, formed like पेतुः (8.35) with e and no reduplicated syllable. √नश् means both ‘perish’ and ‘be lost, vanish’; with 8.40 (‘seeing the Daityas intent on flight’) the sense is ‘they made off, disappeared’.

  • मर्दयन्तम् mardayantam

    Present participle of the causative stem मर्दय- of √मृद् ‘crush’, accusative singular agreeing with the collective मातृगणम्.

Anvaya · prose order

इति क्रुद्धं महासुरान् मर्दयन्तं मातृगणं दृष्ट्वा देवारिसैनिकाः विविधैः अभ्युपायैः नेशुः।

iti kruddhaṃ mahāsurān mardayantaṃ mātṛgaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā devārisainikāḥ vividhaiḥ abhyupāyaiḥ neśuḥ |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The rout of the common soldiers. The word for the Mothers is now collective — मातृगण, ‘the band of Mothers’ — and the asura rank and file disappear, as they will again in 9.41 (केचिद्विनेशुरसुराः केचिन्नष्टा महाहवात्).

Verse 8.40

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

पलायनपरान् दृष्ट्वा दैत्यान् मातृगणार्दितान्।योद्धुमभ्याययौ क्रुद्धो रक्तबीजो महासुरः॥⁠४०⁠॥

palāyanaparān dṛṣṭvā daityān mātṛgaṇārditān |yoddhumabhyāyayau kruddho raktabījo mahāsuraḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

पलायनपरान् · दृष्ट्वा · दैत्यान् · मातृगणार्दितान्योद्धुम् · अभ्याययौ · क्रुद्धः · रक्तबीजः · महासुरः

palāyanaparān · dṛṣṭvā · daityān · mātṛgaṇārditānyoddhum · abhyāyayau · kruddhaḥ · raktabījaḥ · mahāsuraḥ

Word by word

  1. पलायनपरान्palāyanaparānintent on flightm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दैत्यान्
  2. दृष्ट्वाdṛṣṭvāhaving seen√दृश् + क्त्वा (absolutive)
  3. दैत्यान्daityānthe Daityasm. acc. pl. of दैत्य; object of दृष्ट्वा
  4. मातृगणार्दितान्mātṛgaṇārditānharried by the band of Mothersm. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies दैत्यान्
  5. योद्धुम्yoddhumto fight√युध् (divādi, 4Ā ‘to fight’) + तुमुन् (infinitive of purpose)
  6. अभ्याययौabhyāyayaucame forwardअभि + आ + √या (adādi, 2P ‘to go’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  7. क्रुद्धःkruddhaḥenraged√क्रुध् + क्त, m. nom. sg.; qualifies रक्तबीजः
  8. रक्तबीजःraktabījaḥRaktabījam. nom. sg.; compound used as a proper name (see Samāsa); subject
  9. महासुरःmahāsuraḥthe great asuram. nom. sg.; karmadhāraya (महान् असुरः); in apposition to रक्तबीजः

Translation

Seeing the Daityas, harried by the band of Mothers, intent on flight, the great asura Raktabīja, enraged, came forward to fight.

In readable EnglishWhen he saw the Daityas, harried by the Mothers, turning to flee, the great asura Raktabīja advanced in fury to fight.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • योद्धुमभ्याययौ yoddhumabhyāyayau → योद्धुम् + अभ्याययौ

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    Before a vowel the final m stays m and is written joined to the next word.

  • क्रुद्धो रक्तबीजो महासुरः kruddho raktabījo mahāsuraḥ → क्रुद्धः + रक्तबीजः + महासुरः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before the voiced r and m becomes -o, twice.

  • मातृगणार्दितान् mātṛgaṇārditān → मातृगण + अर्दितान्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • पलायनपरान् palāyanaparān

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    पलायन + पर
    Vigraha
    पलायनं परं (प्रधानं) येषां ते, तान् palāyanaṃ paraṃ (pradhānaṃ) yeṣāṃ te, tān
    Literally
    “those for whom flight is the chief thing”
    In the verse
    bent on running away

  • मातृगणार्दितान् mātṛgaṇārditān

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (मातृगण as in 8.39)

    Members
    मातृगण + अर्दित
    Vigraha
    मातृगणेन अर्दिताः, तान् mātṛgaṇena arditāḥ, tān
    Literally
    “afflicted by the band of Mothers”
    In the verse
    routed by the Mātṛkās

  • रक्तबीजः raktabījaḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    रक्त + बीज
    Vigraha
    रक्तं बीजं यस्य सः raktaṃ bījaṃ yasya saḥ
    Literally
    “he whose blood is seed”
    In the verse
    the asura from each drop of whose blood another rises (8.41)
Morphology & anvaya

  • अभ्याययौ abhyāyayau

    Perfect of √या (ययौ) with two prefixes: अभि + आ + ययौ → अभ्याययौ (अभि’s i becomes y before आ, इको यणचि 6.1.77). The same verb brought the Mothers to the field (8.17 अभ्याययौ).

  • योद्धुम् yoddhum

    Infinitive of √युध्: युध् + तुम् → योद्धुम् (guṇa; dh + t → ddh).

Anvaya · prose order

मातृगणार्दितान् दैत्यान् पलायनपरान् दृष्ट्वा क्रुद्धः रक्तबीजः महासुरः योद्धुम् अभ्याययौ।

mātṛgaṇārditān daityān palāyanaparān dṛṣṭvā kruddhaḥ raktabījaḥ mahāsuraḥ yoddhum abhyāyayau |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Raktabīja enters, and the chapter takes its name from him (रक्तबीजवधः). His name is explained by the verse that follows: his blood is seed.

Verse 8.41

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

रक्तबिन्दुर्यदा भूमौ पतत्यस्य शरीरतः।समुत्पतति मेदिन्यां तत्प्रमाणस्तदासुरः॥⁠४१⁠॥

raktabinduryadā bhūmau patatyasya śarīrataḥ |samutpatati medinyāṃ tatpramāṇastadāsuraḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

रक्तबिन्दुः · यदा · भूमौ · पतति · अस्य · शरीरतःसमुत्पतति · मेदिन्याम् · तत्प्रमाणः · तदा · असुरः

raktabinduḥ · yadā · bhūmau · patati · asya · śarīrataḥsamutpatati · medinyām · tatpramāṇaḥ · tadā · asuraḥ

Word by word

  1. रक्तबिन्दुःraktabinduḥa drop of bloodm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of पतति
  2. यदाyadāwheneverindeclinable (relative adverb of time); correlative तदा
  3. भूमौbhūmauon the groundf. loc. sg. of भूमि (i-stem)
  4. पततिpatatifalls√पत् (bhvādi, 1P), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  5. अस्यasyahispronoun इदम्, m. gen. sg.; with शरीरतः
  6. शरीरतःśarīrataḥfrom the bodyindeclinable: शरीर + taddhita -तस् (ablative sense)
  7. समुत्पततिsamutpatatisprings upसम् + उद् + √पत्, laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  8. मेदिन्याम्medinyāmon the earthf. loc. sg. of मेदिनी
  9. तत्प्रमाणःtatpramāṇaḥof his sizem. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies असुरः
  10. तदाtadāthenindeclinable (correlative adverb of time)
  11. असुरःasuraḥan asuram. nom. sg.; subject of समुत्पतति

Translation

Whenever a drop of blood falls from his body to the ground, then an asura of his size springs up on the earth.

In readable EnglishWhenever a drop of his blood fell to the ground, an asura of his own stature sprang up from the earth.

The Sanskrit present of general truth is rendered as a past, to keep the narrative tense.

Textual verification / uncertain: Variant (corrections.json): for the Gita Press तत्प्रमाणस्तदासुरः, GRETIL and Freund read तत्प्रमाणो महासुरः (‘a great asura of his size’). The Gita Press reading is kept; the sense is the same, only तदा (the correlative of यदा) is replaced by महा-.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • रक्तबिन्दुर्यदा raktabinduryadā → रक्तबिन्दुः + यदा

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After u the visarga is sounded r before the voiced y.

  • पतत्यस्य patatyasya → पतति + अस्य

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    i becomes y before a.

  • तत्प्रमाणस्तदासुरः tatpramāṇastadāsuraḥ → तत्प्रमाणः + तदा + असुरः

    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    Visarga before t becomes s; तदा + असुरः → तदासुरः.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • रक्तबिन्दुः raktabinduḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    रक्त + बिन्दु
    Vigraha
    रक्तस्य बिन्दुः raktasya binduḥ
    Literally
    “a drop of blood”
    In the verse
    each single drop

  • तत्प्रमाणः tatpramāṇaḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    तद् + प्रमाण
    Vigraha
    तस्य प्रमाणम् इव प्रमाणं यस्य सः tasya pramāṇam iva pramāṇaṃ yasya saḥ
    Literally
    “he whose measure is (like) his”
    In the verse
    as big and as strong as Raktabīja himself
Morphology & anvaya

  • पतति patati

    Present tense for a standing fact: ‘whenever a drop falls, an asura springs up’. The narrative pauses to state the rule of his nature.

  • शरीरतः śarīrataḥ

    The taddhita -तस् gives an indeclinable with ablative meaning: शरीरतः = शरीरात्, ‘from the body’.

Anvaya · prose order

यदा अस्य शरीरतः रक्तबिन्दुः भूमौ पतति, तदा मेदिन्यां तत्प्रमाणः असुरः समुत्पतति।

yadā asya śarīrataḥ raktabinduḥ bhūmau patati, tadā medinyāṃ tatpramāṇaḥ asuraḥ samutpatati |

A यदा … तदा sentence in the present: condition and consequence.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The verse states Raktabīja’s peculiar power: every drop of blood that reaches the earth becomes another asura his equal. The Devī Māhātmya gives the rule without explaining where it came from.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: violence that only spills more of what it attacks multiplies it. The episode is often read today as a picture of habits and desires that grow each time they are fought on their own terms.

Verse 8.42

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

युयुधे स गदापाणिरिन्द्रशक्त्या महासुरः।ततश्चैन्द्री स्ववज्रेण रक्तबीजमताडयत्॥⁠४२⁠॥

yuyudhe sa gadāpāṇirindraśaktyā mahāsuraḥ |tataścaindrī svavajreṇa raktabījamatāḍayat ||
