युयुधे · सः · गदापाणिः · इन्द्रशक्त्या · महासुरःततः · च · ऐन्द्री · स्ववज्रेण · रक्तबीजम् · अताडयत्

yuyudhe · saḥ · gadāpāṇiḥ · indraśaktyā · mahāsuraḥtataḥ · ca · aindrī · svavajreṇa · raktabījam · atāḍayat

Word by word

  1. युयुधेyuyudhefought√युध् (divādi, 4Ā ‘to fight’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada
  2. सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.
  3. गदापाणिःgadāpāṇiḥmace in handm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies सः
  4. इन्द्रशक्त्याindraśaktyāwith Indra’s power (Aindrī)f. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrumental of association with युयुधे (‘fought with’)
  5. महासुरःmahāsuraḥthe great asuram. nom. sg.; karmadhāraya (as in 8.40); in apposition to सः
  6. ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb, -तस्)
  7. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  8. ऐन्द्रीaindrīAindrīf. nom. sg. of ऐन्द्री (taddhita from इन्द्र); subject
  9. स्ववज्रेणsvavajreṇawith her own thunderboltm. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument
  10. रक्तबीजम्raktabījamRaktabījam. acc. sg.; object of अताडयत्
  11. अताडयत्atāḍayatstruck√तड् (curādi, 10 ‘to strike’, stem ताडय-), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

He, the great asura, mace in hand, fought with the power of Indra; and then Aindrī struck Raktabīja with her own thunderbolt.

In readable EnglishMace in hand, the great asura fought with Indra’s power, and Aindrī struck Raktabīja with her thunderbolt.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • स गदापाणिरिन्द्र sa gadāpāṇirindra → सः + गदापाणिः + इन्द्र…

    visarga sandhi · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    सः drops its visarga before a consonant; गदापाणिः, after i, is sounded r before the vowel of इन्द्र.

  • ततश्चैन्द्री tataścaindrī → ततः + च + ऐन्द्री

    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    Visarga before c becomes ś; च + ऐन्द्री: a + ai → ai.

  • रक्तबीजमताडयत् raktabījamatāḍayat → रक्तबीजम् + अताडयत्

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    The m stays and is written joined.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • गदापाणिः gadāpāṇiḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    गदा + पाणि
    Vigraha
    गदा पाणौ यस्य सः gadā pāṇau yasya saḥ
    Literally
    “he in whose hand is a mace”
    In the verse
    armed with a mace

  • इन्द्रशक्त्या indraśaktyā

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    इन्द्र + शक्ति
    Vigraha
    इन्द्रस्य शक्तिः, तया indrasya śaktiḥ, tayā
    Literally
    “with the power of Indra”
    In the verse
    with Aindrī, named in the next line

  • स्ववज्रेण svavajreṇa

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    स्व + वज्र
    Vigraha
    स्वस्य वज्रः, तेन svasya vajraḥ, tena
    Literally
    “with the thunderbolt of her own”
    In the verse
    with her own vajra
Morphology & anvaya

  • युयुधे yuyudhe

    Perfect of √युध् in the ātmanepada, as the root requires: reduplicated यु-युध्-, ending -ए. Contrast the parasmaipada युयुधुः in 8.45.

  • अताडयत् atāḍayat

    Imperfect of the curādi stem ताडय- (√तड्): augment अ-, ending -त्.

Anvaya · prose order

सः गदापाणिः महासुरः इन्द्रशक्त्या (सह) युयुधे; ततः च ऐन्द्री स्ववज्रेण रक्तबीजम् अताडयत्।

saḥ gadāpāṇiḥ mahāsuraḥ indraśaktyā (saha) yuyudhe; tataḥ ca aindrī svavajreṇa raktabījam atāḍayat |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Raktabīja’s first opponent is Aindrī. Her blow with the vajra draws the first blood, and the next verse shows what it produces.

Verse 8.43

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

कुलिशेनाहतस्याशु बहु सुस्राव शोणितम्।समुत्तस्थुस्ततो योधास्तद्रूपास्तत्पराक्रमाः॥⁠४३⁠॥

kuliśenāhatasyāśu bahu susrāva śoṇitam |samuttasthustato yodhāstadrūpāstatparākramāḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

कुलिशेन · आहतस्य · आशु · बहु · सुस्राव · शोणितम्समुत्तस्थुः · ततः · योधाः · तद्रूपाः · तत्पराक्रमाः

kuliśena · āhatasya · āśu · bahu · susrāva · śoṇitamsamuttasthuḥ · tataḥ · yodhāḥ · tadrūpāḥ · tatparākramāḥ

Word by word

  1. कुलिशेनkuliśenaby the thunderboltm./n. instr. sg. of कुलिश; instrument with आहतस्य
  2. आहतस्यāhatasyaof him (who was) struckआ + √हन् (adādi, 2P) + क्त (past passive participle), m. gen. sg.; ‘from him, struck …’
  3. आशुāśuquickly, at onceindeclinable (adverb)
  4. बहुbahumuchn. nom. sg. of बहु; qualifies शोणितम्
  5. सुस्रावsusrāvaflowed√स्रु (bhvādi, 1P ‘to flow’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  6. शोणितम्śoṇitambloodn. nom. sg. of शोणित; subject of सुस्राव
  7. समुत्तस्थुःsamuttasthuḥrose upसम् + उद् + √स्था (bhvādi, 1P ‘to stand’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  8. ततःtataḥfrom it, thereuponindeclinable (-तस्: ‘from that’)
  9. योधाःyodhāḥwarriorsm. nom. pl. of योध; subject of समुत्तस्थुः
  10. तद्रूपाःtadrūpāḥof his formm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies योधाः
  11. तत्पराक्रमाःtatparākramāḥof his prowessm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies योधाः

Translation

From him, struck by the thunderbolt, much blood flowed at once; from it rose up warriors of his form and his prowess.

In readable EnglishStruck by the thunderbolt, he bled at once and freely, and from that blood rose warriors with his form and his strength.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • कुलिशेनाहतस्याशु kuliśenāhatasyāśu → कुलिशेन + आहतस्य + आशु

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā, twice.

  • समुत्तस्थुस्ततो योधास्तद्रूपास्तत्पराक्रमाः samuttasthustato yodhāstadrūpāstatparākramāḥ → समुत्तस्थुः + ततः + योधाः + तद्रूपाः + तत्पराक्रमाः

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    Visarga before t becomes s (three times); ततः before the voiced y becomes ततो.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • तद्रूपाः tadrūpāḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    तद् + रूप
    Vigraha
    तस्य रूपम् इव रूपं येषां ते tasya rūpam iva rūpaṃ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those whose form is (like) his”
    In the verse
    exact likenesses of Raktabīja

  • तत्पराक्रमाः tatparākramāḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    तद् + पराक्रम
    Vigraha
    तस्य पराक्रमः इव पराक्रमः येषां ते tasya parākramaḥ iva parākramaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those whose valour is (like) his”
    In the verse
    as strong in battle as he
Morphology & anvaya

  • सुस्राव susrāva

    Perfect of √स्रु: reduplication सु-स्राव, with vṛddhi of the root vowel in the third singular.

  • समुत्तस्थुः samuttasthuḥ

    Perfect of √स्था (तस्थुः ‘they stood’) with सम् + उद्: उद् + स्था loses the s of the root (उत्था-), so उत्-तस्थुः. ‘They rose up together’.

  • आहतस्य āhatasya

    Genitive of the past participle, without a noun: ‘of (him) struck’. It names the source of the blood.

Anvaya · prose order

कुलिशेन आहतस्य (तस्य) आशु बहु शोणितं सुस्राव; ततः तद्रूपाः तत्पराक्रमाः योधाः समुत्तस्थुः।

kuliśena āhatasya (tasya) āśu bahu śoṇitaṃ susrāva; tataḥ tadrūpāḥ tatparākramāḥ yodhāḥ samuttasthuḥ |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The rule of 8.41 is now seen working: the first wound fills the field with Raktabīja’s doubles. ततः can be ‘then’ or ‘from it’; both fit, since the warriors rise from the blood.

Verse 8.44

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

यावन्तः पतितास्तस्य शरीराद्रक्तबिन्दवः।तावन्तः पुरुषा जातास्तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः॥⁠४४⁠॥

yāvantaḥ patitāstasya śarīrādraktabindavaḥ |tāvantaḥ puruṣā jātāstadvīryabalavikramāḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यावन्तः · पतिताः · तस्य · शरीरात् · रक्तबिन्दवःतावन्तः · पुरुषाः · जाताः · तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः

yāvantaḥ · patitāḥ · tasya · śarīrāt · raktabindavaḥtāvantaḥ · puruṣāḥ · jātāḥ · tadvīryabalavikramāḥ

Word by word

  1. यावन्तःyāvantaḥas many asrelative quantitative यावत्, m. nom. pl.; correlative तावन्तः
  2. पतिताःpatitāḥfell√पत् + क्त (past participle), m. nom. pl.; used as the predicate (finite past)
  3. तस्यtasyahispronoun तद्, m. gen. sg.
  4. शरीरात्śarīrātfrom the bodyn. abl. sg. of शरीर
  5. रक्तबिन्दवःraktabindavaḥdrops of bloodm. nom. pl.; compound (ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, as in 8.41); subject
  6. तावन्तःtāvantaḥso manycorrelative quantitative तावत्, m. nom. pl.
  7. पुरुषाःpuruṣāḥmen, beingsm. nom. pl. of पुरुष; subject of जाताः
  8. जाताःjātāḥwere born√जन् (divādi, 4Ā ‘to be born’) + क्त, m. nom. pl.; used as the predicate
  9. तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाःtadvīryabalavikramāḥwith his valour, strength and prowessm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies पुरुषाः

Translation

As many drops of blood as fell from his body, so many men were born with his valour, strength and prowess.

In readable EnglishFor every drop of blood that fell from his body a warrior was born, with his valour, his strength and his prowess.

‘For every … a’ renders the correlatives ‘as many … so many’.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • पतितास्तस्य patitāstasya → पतिताः + तस्य

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    Visarga before t becomes s; so also जातास्तद्वीर्य…

  • शरीराद्रक्तबिन्दवः śarīrādraktabindavaḥ → शरीरात् + रक्तबिन्दवः

    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final t becomes d before the voiced r.

  • पुरुषा जाता puruṣā jātā → पुरुषाः + जाताः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    -āḥ loses its visarga before a voiced consonant.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः tadvīryabalavikramāḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing an itaretara dvandva (वीर्यबलविक्रम)

    Members
    तद् + वीर्य + बल + विक्रम
    Vigraha
    वीर्यं च बलं च विक्रमः च वीर्यबलविक्रमाः; तस्य वीर्यबलविक्रमाः इव वीर्यबलविक्रमाः येषां ते vīryaṃ ca balaṃ ca vikramaḥ ca vīryabalavikramāḥ; tasya vīryabalavikramāḥ iva vīryabalavikramāḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those whose valour, strength and prowess are (like) his”
    In the verse
    his equals in every power
Morphology & anvaya

  • यावन्तः yāvantaḥ

    यावत् … तावत् ‘as many … so many’, declined like a participle in -vat: nominative plural यावन्तः, तावन्तः.

  • जाताः jātāḥ

    Past participle of √जन् (जा-त) used as a finite verb, with अभवन् understood.

Anvaya · prose order

तस्य शरीरात् यावन्तः रक्तबिन्दवः पतिताः, तावन्तः तद्वीर्यबलविक्रमाः पुरुषाः जाताः।

tasya śarīrāt yāvantaḥ raktabindavaḥ patitāḥ, tāvantaḥ tadvīryabalavikramāḥ puruṣāḥ jātāḥ |

A यावत् … तावत् sentence of quantity.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The narrator states the arithmetic of the danger: one drop, one Raktabīja. Each copy has not only his shape (8.43) but his full power.

Verse 8.45

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

ते चापि युयुधुस्तत्र पुरुषा रक्तसम्भवाः।समं मातृभिरत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम्॥⁠४५⁠॥

te cāpi yuyudhustatra puruṣā raktasambhavāḥ |samaṃ mātṛbhiratyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ते · च · अपि · युयुधुः · तत्र · पुरुषाः · रक्तसम्भवाःसमम् · मातृभिः · अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम्

te · ca · api · yuyudhuḥ · tatra · puruṣāḥ · raktasambhavāḥsamam · mātṛbhiḥ · atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam

Word by word

  1. तेtetheypronoun तद्, m. nom. pl.
  2. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  3. अपिapitooindeclinable (particle of inclusion)
  4. युयुधुःyuyudhuḥfought√युध् (divādi, 4Ā), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, in a parasmaipada form
  5. तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place)
  6. पुरुषाःpuruṣāḥmen, warriorsm. nom. pl. of पुरुष; in apposition to ते
  7. रक्तसम्भवाःraktasambhavāḥborn of the bloodm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies पुरुषाः
  8. समम्samamwith, together withindeclinable, governs the instrumental
  9. मातृभिःmātṛbhiḥthe Mothersf. instr. pl. of मातृ (ṛ-stem); with समम्
  10. अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम्atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇammost terribly, with the fall of very fierce weaponsn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); used adverbially (kriyāviśeṣaṇa) with युयुधुः

Translation

And those men too, born of the blood, fought there with the Mothers, most terribly with the fall of very fierce weapons.

In readable EnglishThe warriors born of the blood fought the Mothers too, a battle made terrible by the crash of savage weapons.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • चापि cāpi → च + अपि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

  • युयुधुस्तत्र yuyudhustatra → युयुधुः + तत्र

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    Visarga before t becomes s.

  • मातृभिरत्युग्र mātṛbhiratyugra → मातृभिः + अत्युग्र…

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After i the visarga is sounded r before a vowel.

  • पातातिभीषणम् pātātibhīṣaṇam → पात + अतिभीषणम्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā; likewise अति + उग्र → अत्युग्र by इको यणचि (6.1.77).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • रक्तसम्भवाः raktasambhavāḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    रक्त + सम्भव
    Vigraha
    रक्तात् सम्भवः येषां ते raktāt sambhavaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those whose origin is from blood”
    In the verse
    born of Raktabīja’s blood

  • अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम् atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa and a karmadhāraya (अत्युग्रशस्त्र, whose first member अत्युग्र is a prādi compound)

    Members
    अति + उग्र + शस्त्र + पात + अति + भीषण
    Vigraha
    अत्युग्राणि शस्त्राणि अत्युग्रशस्त्राणि; तेषां पातः अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातः; तेन अतिभीषणम् atyugrāṇi śastrāṇi atyugraśastrāṇi; teṣāṃ pātaḥ atyugraśastrapātaḥ; tena atibhīṣaṇam
    Literally
    “exceedingly terrible through the falling of exceedingly fierce weapons”
    In the verse
    a fight made dreadful by a rain of savage blows
    Alternative
    It may also qualify an understood cognate object युद्धम् (‘fought a battle most terrible …’); the meaning is the same.
Morphology & anvaya

  • युयुधुः yuyudhuḥ

    √युध् is an ātmanepada root (8.42 युयुधे); the classical third plural would be युयुधिरे. युयुधुः, with the parasmaipada ending, is an epic (ārṣa) form that the Purāṇas use freely.

  • अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणम् atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇam

    A long compound in the neuter accusative singular serving as an adverb of manner: ‘(fought) in a manner most terrible with …’.

Anvaya · prose order

ते रक्तसम्भवाः पुरुषाः च अपि तत्र मातृभिः समम् अत्युग्रशस्त्रपातातिभीषणं युयुधुः।

te raktasambhavāḥ puruṣāḥ ca api tatra mātṛbhiḥ samam atyugraśastrapātātibhīṣaṇaṃ yuyudhuḥ |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The doubles do not merely appear; they fight, and the Mothers now face an army that grows with every wound they inflict.

Verse 8.46

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

पुनश्च वज्रपातेन क्षतमस्य शिरो यदा।ववाह रक्तं पुरुषास्ततो जाताः सहस्रशः॥⁠४६⁠॥

punaśca vajrapātena kṣatamasya śiro yadā |vavāha raktaṃ puruṣāstato jātāḥ sahasraśaḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

पुनः · च · वज्रपातेन · क्षतम् · अस्य · शिरः · यदाववाह · रक्तम् · पुरुषाः · ततः · जाताः · सहस्रशः

punaḥ · ca · vajrapātena · kṣatam · asya · śiraḥ · yadāvavāha · raktam · puruṣāḥ · tataḥ · jātāḥ · sahasraśaḥ

Word by word

  1. पुनःpunaḥagainindeclinable (adverb; its visarga stands for r)
  2. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  3. वज्रपातेनvajrapātenaby the fall of the thunderboltm. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument with क्षतम्
  4. क्षतम्kṣatamwounded√क्षण् (tanādi, 8U ‘to wound’) + क्त, n. nom. sg.; predicate of शिरः
  5. अस्यasyahispronoun इदम्, m. gen. sg.
  6. शिरःśiraḥheadn. nom. sg. of शिरस् (s-stem); subject
  7. यदाyadāwhenindeclinable (relative adverb of time)
  8. ववाहvavāhapoured out, streamed with√वह् (bhvādi, 1U ‘to carry, flow’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; subject शिरः
  9. रक्तम्raktambloodn. acc. sg. of रक्त; object of ववाह
  10. पुरुषाःpuruṣāḥmen, warriorsm. nom. pl. of पुरुष; subject of जाताः
  11. ततःtataḥfrom thatindeclinable (-तस्)
  12. जाताःjātāḥwere born√जन् + क्त, m. nom. pl.; used as the predicate
  13. सहस्रशःsahasraśaḥby thousandsindeclinable (taddhita -शस्, distributive)

Translation

And again, when his head was wounded by the fall of the thunderbolt, it poured out blood; from that men were born by thousands.

In readable EnglishAgain the thunderbolt struck and wounded his head; the blood poured out, and from it warriors were born in their thousands.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • पुनश्च punaśca → पुनः + च

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)

    Visarga before c becomes ś.

  • क्षतमस्य शिरो यदा kṣatamasya śiro yadā → क्षतम् + अस्य + शिरः + यदा

    final m before a vowel; visarga sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    क्षतम् stays m before the vowel; शिरः before the voiced y becomes शिरो.

  • पुरुषास्ततो जाताः puruṣāstato jātāḥ → पुरुषाः + ततः + जाताः

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); हशि च (6.1.114)

    पुरुषाः before t → पुरुषास्; ततः before the voiced j → ततो.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • वज्रपातेन vajrapātena

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    वज्र + पात
    Vigraha
    वज्रस्य पातः, तेन vajrasya pātaḥ, tena
    Literally
    “by the fall of the thunderbolt”
    In the verse
    by a stroke of Aindrī’s vajra
Morphology & anvaya

  • ववाह vavāha

    Perfect of √वह् with reduplication व-वाह. Used here of the wounded head ‘carrying’ blood, i.e. streaming with it.

  • क्षतम् kṣatam

    Past participle of √क्षण् (the nasal is lost before -त): क्षत ‘wounded’, here a predicate with आसीत् understood.

Anvaya · prose order

पुनः च यदा वज्रपातेन अस्य शिरः क्षतम् (आसीत्), (तदा तत्) रक्तं ववाह; ततः सहस्रशः पुरुषाः जाताः।

punaḥ ca yadā vajrapātena asya śiraḥ kṣatam (āsīt), (tadā tat) raktaṃ vavāha; tataḥ sahasraśaḥ puruṣāḥ jātāḥ |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

A second stroke of the vajra, to the head this time. The numbers rise from hundreds to thousands: each blow makes the problem worse.

Verse 8.47

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

वैष्णवी समरे चैनं चक्रेणाभिजघान ह।गदया ताडयामास ऐन्द्री तमसुरेश्वरम्॥⁠४७⁠॥

vaiṣṇavī samare cainaṃ cakreṇābhijaghāna ha |gadayā tāḍayāmāsa aindrī tamasureśvaram ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

वैष्णवी · समरे · च · एनम् · चक्रेण · अभिजघान · हगदया · ताडयामास · ऐन्द्री · तम् · असुरेश्वरम्

vaiṣṇavī · samare · ca · enam · cakreṇa · abhijaghāna · hagadayā · tāḍayāmāsa · aindrī · tam · asureśvaram

Word by word

  1. वैष्णवीvaiṣṇavīVaiṣṇavīf. nom. sg.; subject of अभिजघान
  2. समरेsamarein the battlem. loc. sg. of समर
  3. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  4. एनम्enamhimpronoun एनद् (the unemphatic ‘him’ of renewed reference), m. acc. sg.; object
  5. चक्रेणcakreṇawith the discusn. instr. sg. of चक्र; instrument
  6. अभिजघानabhijaghānastruckअभि + √हन् (adādi, 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  7. हhaindeedindeclinable, emphatic particle (often a verse-filler)
  8. गदयाgadayāwith a macef. instr. sg. of गदा; instrument
  9. ताडयामासtāḍayāmāsastruck√तड् (curādi, 10, stem ताडय-), periphrastic liṭ (perfect, with आम् + आस), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  10. ऐन्द्रीaindrīAindrīf. nom. sg.; subject of ताडयामास
  11. तम्tamthatpronoun तद्, m. acc. sg.; qualifies असुरेश्वरम्
  12. असुरेश्वरम्asureśvaramlord of the asurasm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of ताडयामास

Translation

And Vaiṣṇavī indeed struck him in battle with the discus; Aindrī struck that lord of the asuras with a mace.

In readable EnglishVaiṣṇavī struck him in battle with her discus, and Aindrī struck the asura lord with a mace.

Textual verification / uncertain: गदया with ऐन्द्री: Aindrī’s weapon elsewhere in the chapter is the vajra. corrections.json logs no variant for this verse; the mace is translated as written.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • चैनं cainaṃ → च + एनम्

    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    a + e → ai. The final m of एनम् becomes anusvāra before the c of चक्रेण (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

  • चक्रेणाभिजघान cakreṇābhijaghāna → चक्रेण + अभिजघान

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

  • ताडयामास ऐन्द्री tāḍayāmāsa aindrī → ताडयामास + ऐन्द्री

    hiatus (no sandhi) at the pāda break · an exception to वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88): metrical licence

    Regular sandhi would give ताडयामासैन्द्री; the text keeps the words apart at the pāda boundary. Declared in the sandhi exceptions.

  • असुरेश्वरम् asureśvaram → असुर + ईश्वरम्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + ī → e.

Left unjoined

  • ताडयामास | ऐन्द्री — Hiatus at the pāda break (गदया ताडयामास is a full pāda of eight syllables): the a of ताडयामास is not fused with the ai of ऐन्द्री (which would give ताडयामासैन्द्री). A licence of epic verse, which may treat the pāda boundary as a pause; the text is printed so in the Gita Press.
Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • असुरेश्वरम् asureśvaram

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    असुर + ईश्वर
    Vigraha
    असुराणाम् ईश्वरः, तम् asurāṇām īśvaraḥ, tam
    Literally
    “the lord of the asuras”
    In the verse
    Raktabīja, as a commander of the asura host
Morphology & anvaya

  • ताडयामास tāḍayāmāsa

    Periphrastic perfect: stem ताडय- + आम् + the perfect of √अस् (आस). Derived stems (causative, curādi) form their perfect this way.

  • एनम् enam

    एनद् is used only in the accusative, instrumental singular and a few other forms, for a person already mentioned: ‘him’ (Raktabīja).

Anvaya · prose order

वैष्णवी च समरे एनं चक्रेण अभिजघान ह; ऐन्द्री तम् असुरेश्वरं गदया ताडयामास।

vaiṣṇavī ca samare enaṃ cakreṇa abhijaghāna ha; aindrī tam asureśvaraṃ gadayā tāḍayāmāsa |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Two Mothers attack together. The text gives Aindrī a mace (गदा) here, where earlier she used her thunderbolt (8.42, 8.46); the Gita Press reading is kept as it stands.

Verse 8.48

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैः।सहस्रशो जगद्व्याप्तं तत्प्रमाणैर्महासुरैः॥⁠४८⁠॥

vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ |sahasraśo jagadvyāptaṃ tatpramāṇairmahāsuraiḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य · रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैःसहस्रशः · जगत् · व्याप्तम् · तत्प्रमाणैः · महासुरैः

vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya · rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥsahasraśaḥ · jagat · vyāptam · tatpramāṇaiḥ · mahāsuraiḥ

Word by word

  1. वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्यvaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasyaof him, split by Vaiṣṇavī’s discusm. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on the रुधिरस्राव- of the next word
  2. रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैःrudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥborn from the flow of bloodm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महासुरैः
  3. सहस्रशःsahasraśaḥby thousandsindeclinable (taddhita -शस्)
  4. जगत्jagatthe worldn. nom. sg. of जगत्; subject of the passive व्याप्तम्
  5. व्याप्तम्vyāptamwas pervaded, filledवि + √आप् (svādi, 5P ‘to reach’) + क्त, n. nom. sg.; predicate (with आसीत् understood)
  6. तत्प्रमाणैःtatpramāṇaiḥof his sizem. instr. pl.; bahuvrīhi (as in 8.41); qualifies महासुरैः
  7. महासुरैःmahāsuraiḥby great asurasm. instr. pl.; karmadhāraya (महान्तः असुराः); agent of व्याप्तम्

Translation

By great asuras of his size, born by thousands from the flow of blood of him who was split by Vaiṣṇavī’s discus, the world was filled.

In readable EnglishFrom the blood that streamed from him when Vaiṣṇavī’s discus split him open, great asuras of his stature were born in thousands and filled the world.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • सहस्रशो जगद्व्याप्तं sahasraśo jagadvyāptaṃ → सहस्रशः + जगत् + व्याप्तम्

    visarga sandhi; consonant sandhi (voicing) · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    -aḥ before the voiced j becomes -o; the final t of जगत् becomes d before the voiced v.

  • तत्प्रमाणैर्महासुरैः tatpramāṇairmahāsuraiḥ → तत्प्रमाणैः + महासुरैः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After ai the visarga is sounded r before the voiced m.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (वैष्णवीचक्र)

    Members
    वैष्णवी + चक्र + भिन्न
    Vigraha
    वैष्णव्याः चक्रं वैष्णवीचक्रम्; तेन भिन्नः, तस्य vaiṣṇavyāḥ cakraṃ vaiṣṇavīcakram; tena bhinnaḥ, tasya
    Literally
    “of the one split by Vaiṣṇavī’s discus”
    In the verse
    of Raktabīja, gashed by the discus of 8.47

  • रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैः rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (रुधिरस्राव)

    Members
    रुधिर + स्राव + सम्भव
    Vigraha
    रुधिरस्य स्रावः रुधिरस्रावः; रुधिरस्रावात् सम्भवः येषां तैः rudhirasya srāvaḥ rudhirasrāvaḥ; rudhirasrāvāt sambhavaḥ yeṣāṃ taiḥ
    Literally
    “by those whose origin is from the flow of blood”
    In the verse
    born of the streaming blood
Morphology & anvaya

  • व्याप्तम् vyāptam

    Past passive participle of वि + √आप्: ‘reached through, pervaded’. With the instrumental agent महासुरैः it makes a passive sentence: ‘the world was filled by great asuras’.

  • वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya

    भिन्न is the past participle of √भिद् ‘split’, with -न instead of -त. The genitive names whose blood it was.

Anvaya · prose order

वैष्णवीचक्रभिन्नस्य (तस्य) रुधिरस्रावसम्भवैः तत्प्रमाणैः महासुरैः सहस्रशः जगत् व्याप्तम् (आसीत्)।

vaiṣṇavīcakrabhinnasya (tasya) rudhirasrāvasambhavaiḥ tatpramāṇaiḥ mahāsuraiḥ sahasraśaḥ jagat vyāptam (āsīt) |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The scale widens from the battlefield to the world (जगत्). The passive construction makes the asuras the agents and the world the thing overwhelmed.

Verse 8.49

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

शक्त्या जघान कौमारी वाराही च तथासिना।माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन रक्तबीजं महासुरम्॥⁠४९⁠॥

śaktyā jaghāna kaumārī vārāhī ca tathāsinā |māheśvarī triśūlena raktabījaṃ mahāsuram ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

शक्त्या · जघान · कौमारी · वाराही · च · तथा · असिनामाहेश्वरी · त्रिशूलेन · रक्तबीजम् · महासुरम्

śaktyā · jaghāna · kaumārī · vārāhī · ca · tathā · asināmāheśvarī · triśūlena · raktabījam · mahāsuram

Word by word

  1. शक्त्याśaktyāwith the spearf. instr. sg. of शक्ति (the weapon, as in 8.34)
  2. जघानjaghānastruck√हन् (adādi, 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; shared by the three subjects
  3. कौमारीkaumārīKaumārīf. nom. sg.; subject
  4. वाराहीvārāhīVārāhīf. nom. sg. of वाराही (taddhita from वराह); subject
  5. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  6. तथाtathālikewiseindeclinable (connective)
  7. असिनाasināwith the swordm. instr. sg. of असि (i-stem)
  8. माहेश्वरीmāheśvarīMāheśvarīf. nom. sg.; subject
  9. त्रिशूलेनtriśūlenawith the tridentn. instr. sg.; compound (bahuvrīhi, as in 8.34)
  10. रक्तबीजम्raktabījamRaktabījam. acc. sg.; bahuvrīhi used as a name (as in 8.40); object of जघान
  11. महासुरम्mahāsuramthe great asuram. acc. sg.; karmadhāraya; in apposition to रक्तबीजम्

Translation

Kaumārī struck the great asura Raktabīja with the spear, and likewise Vārāhī with the sword, Māheśvarī with the trident.

In readable EnglishKaumārī struck the great asura Raktabīja with her spear, Vārāhī with her sword, and Māheśvarī with her trident.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तथासिना tathāsinā → तथा + असिना

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

  • रक्तबीजं महासुरम् raktabījaṃ mahāsuram → रक्तबीजम् + महासुरम्

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant is written as anusvāra.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

No compound in this verse needs analysis.

Morphology & anvaya

  • जघान jaghāna

    As in 8.34: one singular perfect for three singular subjects, each with her own instrument.

Anvaya · prose order

कौमारी शक्त्या, तथा वाराही च असिना, माहेश्वरी त्रिशूलेन रक्तबीजं महासुरं जघान।

kaumārī śaktyā, tathā vārāhī ca asinā, māheśvarī triśūlena raktabījaṃ mahāsuraṃ jaghāna |

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Every Mother now attacks Raktabīja himself, and every blow is another wound. The verse repeats the pattern of 8.34 — three goddesses, three weapons, one verb — but now against the one enemy whose wounds multiply him.

Verse 8.50

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

स चापि गदया दैत्यः सर्वा एवाहनत् पृथक्।मातॄः कोपसमाविष्टो रक्तबीजो महासुरः॥⁠५०⁠॥

sa cāpi gadayā daityaḥ sarvā evāhanat pṛthak |mātṝḥ kopasamāviṣṭo raktabījo mahāsuraḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सः · च · अपि · गदया · दैत्यः · सर्वाः · एव · अहनत् · पृथक्मातॄः · कोपसमाविष्टः · रक्तबीजः · महासुरः

saḥ · ca · api · gadayā · daityaḥ · sarvāḥ · eva · ahanat · pṛthakmātṝḥ · kopasamāviṣṭaḥ · raktabījaḥ · mahāsuraḥ

Word by word

  1. सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.
  2. चcaandindeclinable, conjunctive particle
  3. अपिapifor his part, tooindeclinable (particle of inclusion)
  4. गदयाgadayāwith (his) macef. instr. sg. of गदा
  5. दैत्यःdaityaḥthe Daityam. nom. sg. of दैत्य; in apposition to सः
  6. सर्वाःsarvāḥallpronominal adjective सर्व, f. acc. pl.; qualifies मातॄः
  7. एवevaindeed, every oneindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  8. अहनत्ahanatstruck√हन् (adādi, 2P), laṅ (imperfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada, in a thematic form
  9. पृथक्pṛthakeach separatelyindeclinable (adverb)
  10. मातॄःmātṝḥthe Mothersf. acc. pl. of मातृ (ṛ-stem); object of अहनत्
  11. कोपसमाविष्टःkopasamāviṣṭaḥpossessed by ragem. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रक्तबीजः
  12. रक्तबीजःraktabījaḥRaktabījam. nom. sg.; in apposition to सः
